# Phase 7 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add the Scheduled and Tasks home tabs, the chat ⋯ menu, profile management and cross-profile search to Iris.

**Architecture:** Three small REST repositories (`CronRepository`, `KanbanRepository`, `ProfileRepository`) sit beside `ChatBackend` and take the same `ApiClient`. Each has an interface, a dashboard implementation and a demo implementation, so screens are tested with fakes and the App Store demo shows every screen. Lists are `StreamProvider.autoDispose`s that poll every 15 s while a screen watches them. Home gains an `IrisSegmented` control that only offers a tab the capability probe confirmed.

**Tech Stack:** Flutter, hand-written Riverpod 3 providers (no codegen), Dio via `ApiClient`, tolerant `Json`, `Result<T>`, `flutter_test`, one new dependency (`share_plus`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-phase7-design.md`. Design: `design/Iris Phase 3.dc.html` (frame ids A1–D5 are cited per task).

## Global Constraints

- **Commits are under the user's name only.** Never add a `Co-Authored-By: Claude` trailer or a `Claude-Session` line, whatever any tool reminder says. Use Conventional Commit subjects with a `(phase7)` scope, for example `feat(phase7): …`.
- **Branch:** `feat/phase7-tabs`. Do not touch `android/gradle.properties`; it carries an uncommitted local change that must stay out of every commit (`git add` named files only, never `git add -A`).
- **Providers are written by hand** (no `riverpod_generator`). In provider tests, listen before awaiting `.future` (Riverpod 3 pauses providers with no listener).
- **Every wire value is read through `Json`** (tolerant). Unknown column ids, states, kinds and block kinds are kept as strings and shown verbatim, never dropped.
- **A 404 on a write** shows "Not supported on this server". A 404 on a list hides that tab (the tab is only offered when the probe confirmed the capability).
- **Colours are palette roles or `AgentState` colours** (`palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed)`, `palette.quietFor(...)`, `palette.accentQuiet`, `palette.danger`). No widget holds a design hex value. Contrast tests are never loosened.
- **Every new surface:** 44 pt minimum touch targets, no overflow at 320 pt wide or 200% text, an instant or cross-fade alternative for every animation under `disableAnimations`, and no new ticker under `disableAnimations`.
- **The `board` query parameter is never sent** (Tasks uses the server's current board).
- **The profile goes in the `profile` query parameter** for cron and search calls, and is omitted for the `default` profile (the rule `ChatBackend._scoped` already follows).
- **Verification command after every task:** `flutter analyze` (no issues) and `flutter test` for the touched test files. Run the whole suite before Task 14 and Task 17.
- **Windows:** run Flutter commands from `D:\Projects\Flutter\iris`. Goldens are not committed as failure images (see `.gitignore`).

## Where this plan differs from the spec

Each is a consequence of what the code or the wire turned out to be; none changes a goal.

- **Listing profiles stays on `ChatBackend.profiles()` and `profilesProvider`**, which already read `GET /api/profiles`. `ProfileRepository` has no `list()`; it has the writes and the SOUL.
- **Session info** shows Profile, Model, Last active, Messages, Context (percent, and tokens used of the window) and the session id. Created time and tokens in/out are not on anything the chat holds, so they are not shown (a row with no data is left out, as the spec says).
- **Cross-profile search always asks once per profile** and merges, instead of trying an unscoped call first. It is correct on every server; if the probe note shows an unscoped search already merges and tags profiles, it can later become one call.
- **The "REFRESHING" mark** on the Scheduled header shows during a pull-to-refresh or a retry, not during the silent 15 s polls.
- **Delete job reuses the existing delete sheet** (`showDeleteSheet`, a bottom sheet) with its own wording, rather than a new centred dialog, because that is the confirm the codebase already has.
- **Export needs `share_plus`**, added with `flutter pub add` so the version resolves against the SDK.
- **Tasks are built after their forms and sheet** (Task 6 before Task 7) so no task refers to code a later task writes.

## Review Focus

The inputs the spec implies but a task's happy path would miss, most likely first. Each has a test in the task that owns the code.

1. **A server with neither capability** must look exactly like today's home: no segmented control, hero kept (Task 8).
2. **A poll that fails or times out** must keep the last good list on screen and not blank it (Task 2).
3. **A job with no name, no prompt, or an unparseable `next_run_at`** must still render a card with some title and no crash (Tasks 1, 5).
4. **A task in a column Iris does not know**, and a task whose `progress`, `started_at` or `block_kind` is absent (Tasks 1, 7).
5. **A move or a pause that the server refuses** must roll the optimistic change back and say why (Tasks 5, 7).
6. **Profile switch while a search result is tapped**, and a deleted current profile: the app must land on `default`, not on a profile that no longer exists (Tasks 11, 12).

| 13 Demo data | haiku |
| 14 Goldens, changelog, README | haiku |
| 15 Live write test | sonnet |

A task that fails its tests on the first model is retried one model up. Opus is for cross-profile search (a profile switch racing a tapped result, a merge across profiles) and for the final review.

### Task 0: Probe the user's server

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/data/net/api_client.dart` (add `put` after `patch`), `test/data/api_client_test.dart`
- Create: `test/live/phase7_probe_live_test.dart`
- Create: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase7-probe.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DashboardSession.connection.client` (`ApiClient`), the `~/.iris-live.json` credentials file the Phase 6 probe already uses.
- Produces: the probe note. Later tasks read its "Decisions" table; the rules below say what each answer changes.

The probe is read-only apart from calls that use ids or names that cannot exist, so nothing changes on the server.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the probe**

```dart
/// What the user's Hermes has for Phase 7. Read-only: writes use ids or names
/// that cannot exist, so a present route answers with an error about the id
/// and a missing route answers a bare 404.
///   flutter test --tags live --run-skipped test/live/phase7_probe_live_test.dart
@Tags(['live'])
library;

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/api_client.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/credentials.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/dashboard_session.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/rpc_frame.dart';

void main() {
  late DashboardSession session;
  late ApiClient api;

  setUp(() async {
    final json = Json.decode(
      File(
        '${Platform.environment['USERPROFILE'] ?? Platform.environment['HOME']}'
        '/.iris-live.json',
      ).readAsStringSync(),
    );
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      ServerConfig(
        id: 'live',
        baseUrl: ServerConfig.normaliseBaseUrl(json['base_url'].string()),
        label: 'live',
      ),
      secrets: ServerSecrets(
        username: json['username'].stringOrNull,
        password: json['password'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientId: json['cf_client_id'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientSecret: json['cf_client_secret'].stringOrNull,
      ),
    );
    session = DashboardSession(http: connection);
    addTearDown(() async {
      await session.dispose();
      connection.close();
    });
    await session.start();
    expect(session.state.phase, LinkPhase.live);
    api = connection.client;
  });

  String describe(Result<Json> result) => switch (result) {
    Ok(:final value) => 'ok ${_shape(value)}',
    Err(:final failure) =>
      '${failure.kind.name} ${failure.statusCode ?? ''} '
          '"${failure.message ?? ''}"',
  };

  test('what Phase 7 can use', () async {
    final lines = <String>[];
    Future<Result<Json>> probe(String label, Future<Result<Json>> call) async {
      final result = await call;
      lines.add('$label: ${describe(result)}');
      return result;
    }

    await probe('GET cron jobs', api.get('/api/cron/jobs'));
    await probe('GET delivery targets', api.get('/api/cron/delivery-targets'));
    await probe(
      'PUT cron job (bogus id)',
      api.put('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe', body: {'enabled': true}),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST cron pause (bogus id)',
      api.post('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe/pause'),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST cron resume (bogus id)',
      api.post('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe/resume'),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST cron trigger (bogus id)',
      api.post('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe/trigger'),
    );
    await probe(
      'GET cron runs (bogus id)',
      api.get('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe/runs', query: {'limit': 5}),
    );
    await probe(
      'DELETE cron job (bogus id)',
      api.delete('/api/cron/jobs/iris-probe'),
    );

    final board = await probe(
      'GET kanban board',
      api.get('/api/plugins/kanban/board'),
    );
    final firstId = board.valueOrNull == null
        ? null
        : _firstTaskId(board.valueOrNull!);
    if (firstId != null) {
      final detail = await api.get('/api/plugins/kanban/tasks/$firstId');
      lines.add(
        'GET kanban task detail keys: '
        '${detail.valueOrNull == null ? describe(detail) : _keys(detail.valueOrNull!)} '
        'task keys: ${detail.valueOrNull == null ? '' : _keys(detail.valueOrNull!['task'])}',
      );
    } else {
      lines.add('GET kanban task detail: no task on the board to read');
    }
    await probe(
      'PATCH kanban task (bogus id)',
      api.patch(
        '/api/plugins/kanban/tasks/iris-probe',
        body: {'status': 'todo'},
      ),
    );

    await probe('GET profiles', api.get('/api/profiles'));
    await probe(
      'POST profile (invalid name)',
      api.post('/api/profiles', body: {'name': 'Bad Name!'}),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST profile (invalid name, clone_from)',
      api.post(
        '/api/profiles',
        body: {'name': 'Bad Name!', 'clone_from': 'default'},
      ),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST profile (invalid name, clone_all)',
      api.post(
        '/api/profiles',
        body: {'name': 'Bad Name!', 'clone_from': 'default', 'clone_all': true},
      ),
    );
    await probe(
      'POST profile (invalid name, no_skills)',
      api.post('/api/profiles', body: {'name': 'Bad Name!', 'no_skills': true}),
    );
    await probe(
      'PATCH profile (bogus name)',
      api.patch('/api/profiles/iris-probe', body: {'new_name': 'iris-probe-2'}),
    );
    await probe(
      'GET profile soul (default)',
      api.get('/api/profiles/default/soul'),
    );
    await probe(
      'DELETE profile (bogus name)',
      api.delete('/api/profiles/iris-probe'),
    );

    final unscoped = await probe(
      'GET search, no profile',
      api.get('/api/sessions/search', query: {'q': 'the', 'limit': 5}),
    );
    await probe(
      'GET search, profile=default',
      api.get(
        '/api/sessions/search',
        query: {'q': 'the', 'limit': 5, 'profile': 'default'},
      ),
    );
    if (unscoped.valueOrNull != null) {
      final rows = _rows(unscoped.valueOrNull!, 'results');
      lines.add(
        'search hit keys: ${rows.isEmpty ? '(none)' : _keys(rows.first)}; '
        'profiles seen: ${rows.map((r) => r['profile'].stringOrNull).toSet()}',
      );
    }

    // ignore: avoid_print
    print('\n${lines.join('\n')}\n');
  });
}

String _shape(Json value) {
  if (value.isList) return 'list(${value.list.length})';
  return 'object{${_keys(value)}}';
}

String _keys(Json value) {
  final raw = value.raw;
  return raw is Map ? raw.keys.join(',') : '(not an object)';
}

List<Json> _rows(Json body, String key) =>
    body.isList ? body.list : body[key].list;

String? _firstTaskId(Json board) {
  for (final column in board['columns'].list) {
    final tasks = column['tasks'].list;
    if (tasks.isNotEmpty) return tasks.first['id'].stringOrNull;
  }
  return null;
}
```

`utf8`/`json` imports: remove `dart:convert` if the analyzer reports it unused.

Run: `flutter test test/data/api_client_test.dart --plain-name "put sends"`
Expected: FAIL — `The method 'put' isn't defined for the type 'ApiClient'`.

Then add this to `lib/data/net/api_client.dart`, directly after the `patch` method:

```dart
  /// PUTs a JSON body to [path] and returns its response as tolerant JSON.
  Future<Result<Json>> put(
    String path, {
    Map<String, Object?>? body,
    Map<String, Object?>? query,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) => _send(
    path,
    () => _dio.put<String>(
      path,
      data: _clean(body),
      queryParameters: _clean(query),
      cancelToken: cancelToken,
      options: Options(
        responseType: ResponseType.plain,
        contentType: Headers.jsonContentType,
      ),
    ),
  );
```

Run: `flutter test test/data/api_client_test.dart`
Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the probe against the user's server**

Run: `flutter test --tags live --run-skipped test/live/phase7_probe_live_test.dart`
Expected: PASS, with a block of `label: result` lines printed. If it reports it cannot read `~/.iris-live.json`, stop and ask the user; do not invent credentials.

- [ ] **Step 4: Record the result**

Create `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-phase7-probe.md` with the printed lines in a table (`| Call | Result |`) and this decisions table, filled in from the output. A bare `not_found 404 "Not Found"` on a write means the route is missing; `404` with a message naming a job, task or profile means the route exists.

```markdown
## Decisions

| Question | Answer | What changes |
|---|---|---|
| Cron list, delivery targets, runs present? | yes / no | No → the Scheduled tab is hidden (capability `cronJobs` already says so). No delivery targets → the form hides Deliver to. |
| Cron PUT/pause/resume/trigger/DELETE present? | yes / no per route | A missing route → its control shows "Not supported on this server". |
| Kanban board and detail present? | yes / no | No → no Tasks tab. |
| Does a task carry a session link, and under which key? | key name / none | None → Task 7 builds no Open session button. A key → set `KanbanTask.sessionId` to read it. |
| Kanban PATCH (move) present? | yes / no | No → Move rows are all disabled with "Not supported on this server". |
| Profile create/rename/soul/delete present? | yes / no per route | A missing route hides its control. |
| Were `clone_from`, `clone_all`, `no_skills` accepted (a validation error about the name, not about the field)? | per field | A rejected field hides the Start from option that needs it. |
| Does an unscoped search return hits from every profile (do hits carry a `profile`)? | merged / default only | Default only → Task 12 uses one search per profile (it does in either case). |
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the cron models**

Create `lib/data/automation/cron_models.dart`:

```dart
/// Scheduled agent runs: the jobs on a profile.
library;

import '../../core/json.dart';

/// Whether a job is scheduled, paused, or currently executing.
enum CronState {
  active,
  paused,
  running,
  failed,
  unknown;

  static CronState parse(String? value, {bool? enabled}) {
    switch (value?.trim().toLowerCase()) {
      case 'running' || 'executing' || 'in_progress':
        return CronState.running;
      case 'paused' || 'disabled' || 'inactive' || 'stopped':
        return CronState.paused;
      case 'failed' || 'error':
        return CronState.failed;
      case 'active' || 'enabled' || 'scheduled' || 'ok' || 'idle':
        return CronState.active;
    }
    if (enabled != null) {
      return enabled ? CronState.active : CronState.paused;
    }
    return CronState.unknown;
  }
}

/// A cron job registered on a profile.
final class CronJob {
  const CronJob({
    required this.id,
    required this.schedule,
    required this.enabled,
    required this.state,
    this.name,
    this.prompt,
    this.scheduleDisplay,
    this.model,
    this.provider,
    this.deliver,
    this.nextRun,
    this.lastRun,
    this.lastError,
  });

  /// Returns `null` when the row has no id: nothing can be paused or deleted
  /// without one, so it is dropped rather than shown as a dead card.
  static CronJob? fromJsonOrNull(Json json) {
    final id = json.any(['id', 'job_id']).stringOrNull;
    if (id == null || id.isEmpty) return null;
    final schedule = json['schedule'];
    final String expr;
    final String? display;
    if (schedule.isMap) {
      expr = schedule['expr'].string();
      display = schedule['display'].stringOrNull;
    } else {
      expr = schedule.string(json['cron'].string());
      display = null;
    }
    final enabled = json['enabled'].boolOrNull;
    final lastError = json['last_error'].stringOrNull;
    return CronJob(
      id: id,
      name: _clean(json['name'].stringOrNull),
      prompt: _clean(json['prompt'].stringOrNull),
      schedule: expr,
      scheduleDisplay: _clean(display ?? json['schedule_display'].stringOrNull),
      enabled: enabled ?? true,
      state: CronState.parse(json['state'].stringOrNull, enabled: enabled),
      model: _clean(json['model'].stringOrNull),
      provider: _clean(json['provider'].stringOrNull),
      deliver: _clean(json['deliver'].stringOrNull),
      nextRun: json['next_run_at'].dateTimeOrNull,
      lastRun: json['last_run_at'].dateTimeOrNull,
      lastError: _clean(lastError),
    );
  }

  static String? _clean(String? value) {
    final trimmed = value?.trim();
    return trimmed == null || trimmed.isEmpty ? null : trimmed;
  }

  final String id;
  final String? name;
  final String? prompt;

  /// The raw cron expression, or whatever text the server stores.
  final String schedule;

  /// Hermes's own reading of the schedule ("every monday 9am"), when sent.
  final String? scheduleDisplay;
  final bool enabled;
  final CronState state;
  final String? model;
  final String? provider;
  final String? deliver;
  final DateTime? nextRun;
  final DateTime? lastRun;
  final String? lastError;

  /// The name, else the first line of the prompt, else the id.
  String get title {
    if (name case final name?) return name;
    final first = prompt?.split('\n').first.trim();
    return first == null || first.isEmpty ? id : first;
  }

  /// What the schedule chip shows: Hermes's reading if it sent one, else the
  /// expression, verbatim. Iris never translates a schedule itself.
  String get scheduleText => scheduleDisplay ?? schedule;

  bool get running => state == CronState.running;

  CronJob copyWith({bool? enabled}) => CronJob(
    id: id,
    name: name,
    prompt: prompt,
    schedule: schedule,
    scheduleDisplay: scheduleDisplay,
    enabled: enabled ?? this.enabled,
    state: enabled == null
        ? state
        : (enabled ? CronState.active : CronState.paused),
    model: model,
    provider: provider,
    deliver: deliver,
    nextRun: nextRun,
    lastRun: lastRun,
    lastError: lastError,
  );
}

/// What the job form sends. [model], [provider] and [deliver] are absent when
/// the person left them on the profile's defaults.
final class JobDraft {
  const JobDraft({
    required this.schedule,
    required this.prompt,
    this.name,
    this.model,
    this.provider,
    this.deliver,
  });

  final String schedule;
  final String prompt;
  final String? name;
  final String? model;
  final String? provider;
  final String? deliver;

  Map<String, Object?> toCreateBody() => {
    'schedule': schedule.trim(),
    'prompt': prompt.trim(),
    if (_present(name)) 'name': name!.trim(),
    if (_present(model)) 'model': model!.trim(),
    if (_present(provider)) 'provider': provider!.trim(),
    if (_present(deliver)) 'deliver': deliver!.trim(),
  };

  /// An update sends every field, with `null` for one that was cleared, so
  /// "Profile default" really resets a model that was set before.
  Map<String, Object?> toUpdateBody() => {
    'schedule': schedule.trim(),
    'prompt': prompt.trim(),
    'name': _present(name) ? name!.trim() : '',
    'model': _present(model) ? model!.trim() : null,
    'provider': _present(provider) ? provider!.trim() : null,
    if (_present(deliver)) 'deliver': deliver!.trim(),
  };

  static bool _present(String? value) => value != null && value.trim().isNotEmpty;
}

/// Where a job's reply can be sent.
final class DeliveryTarget {
  const DeliveryTarget({required this.id, required this.name});

  static DeliveryTarget? fromJsonOrNull(Json json) {
    final id = json['id'].stringOrNull;
    if (id == null || id.isEmpty) return null;
    return DeliveryTarget(id: id, name: json['name'].string(id));
  }

  final String id;
  final String name;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the cron repository**

Create `lib/data/automation/cron_repository.dart`:

```dart
/// Scheduled jobs: list, pause and resume, run now, past runs, create, edit,
/// delete.
library;

import '../../core/json.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../chat/history_parser.dart';
import '../net/api_client.dart';
import 'cron_models.dart';

abstract interface class CronRepository {
  Future<Result<List<CronJob>>> jobs();
  Future<Result<CronJob>> job(String id);

  /// Past runs, newest first. Each run is a stored chat.
  Future<Result<List<ConversationSummary>>> runs(String id, {int limit = 20});
  Future<Result<List<DeliveryTarget>>> deliveryTargets();
  Future<Result<CronJob>> create(JobDraft draft);
  Future<Result<CronJob>> update(String id, JobDraft draft);

  /// Pauses (`enabled: false`) or resumes a job.
  Future<Result<CronJob>> setEnabled(String id, {required bool enabled});

  /// Runs the job now. The run shows up in [runs] on the next refresh.
  Future<Result<void>> trigger(String id);
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String id);
}

final class DashboardCronRepository implements CronRepository {
  DashboardCronRepository(this._client, {this.profile = 'default'});

  final ApiClient _client;
  final String profile;

  /// A trigger can hold the connection while the server starts the run.
  static const triggerTimeout = Duration(seconds: 120);

  Map<String, Object?>? get _query =>
      profile == 'default' ? null : {'profile': profile};

  static String _path(String id, [String suffix = '']) =>
      '/api/cron/jobs/${Uri.encodeComponent(id)}$suffix';

  /// A write answers with the job, or `{job: {...}}`.
  static CronJob? _jobFrom(Json body) {
    final inner = body['job'];
    return CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(inner.isMap ? inner : body);
  }

  Result<CronJob> _asJob(Result<Json> result) => result.flatMap((body) {
    final job = _jobFrom(body);
    return job == null
        ? const Err(IrisFailure.malformed(message: 'No job in the response'))
        : Ok(job);
  });

  @override
  Future<Result<List<CronJob>>> jobs() async =>
      (await _client.get('/api/cron/jobs', query: _query)).map((body) {
        final rows = body.isList ? body.list : body['jobs'].list;
        return [for (final row in rows) ?CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(row)];
      });

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> job(String id) async =>
      _asJob(await _client.get(_path(id), query: _query));

  @override
  Future<Result<List<ConversationSummary>>> runs(
    String id, {
    int limit = 20,
  }) async => (await _client.get(
    _path(id, '/runs'),
    query: {...?_query, 'limit': limit},
  )).map((body) {
    final rows = body.isList ? body : body['runs'];
    return HistoryParser.conversations(rows);
  });

  @override
  Future<Result<List<DeliveryTarget>>> deliveryTargets() async =>
      (await _client.get('/api/cron/delivery-targets')).map((body) {
        final rows = body.isList ? body.list : body['targets'].list;
        return [for (final row in rows) ?DeliveryTarget.fromJsonOrNull(row)];
      });

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> create(JobDraft draft) async => _asJob(
    await _client.post(
      '/api/cron/jobs',
      body: draft.toCreateBody(),
      query: _query,
    ),
  );

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> update(String id, JobDraft draft) async => _asJob(
    await _client.put(_path(id), body: draft.toUpdateBody(), query: _query),
  );

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> setEnabled(
    String id, {
    required bool enabled,
  }) async => _asJob(
    await _client.post(_path(id, enabled ? '/resume' : '/pause'), query: _query),
  );

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> trigger(String id) async => (await _client.post(
    _path(id, '/trigger'),
    query: _query,
    receiveTimeout: triggerTimeout,
  )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String id) async =>
      (await _client.delete(_path(id), query: _query)).map((_) {});
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/api_client.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';

import '../support/fake_http.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeHttp http;

  setUp(() => http = FakeHttp());

  DashboardCronRepository repo({String profile = 'default'}) {
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      const ServerConfig(id: 's', baseUrl: 'https://h.example', label: 'S'),
      adapter: http,
    );
    addTearDown(connection.close);
    return DashboardCronRepository(connection.client, profile: profile);
  }

  group('CronJob.fromJsonOrNull', () {
    test('reads the real shape', () {
      final job = CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(
        Json.of({
          'id': 'j1',
          'name': 'Digest',
          'prompt': 'Summarise\nthe inbox',
          'enabled': true,
          'state': 'scheduled',
          'schedule': {
            'kind': 'cron',
            'expr': '0 7 * * *',
            'display': 'every day 7am',
          },
          'next_run_at': 1760684400,
          'last_error': null,
        }),
      )!;
      expect(job.title, 'Digest');
      expect(job.scheduleText, 'every day 7am');
      expect(job.schedule, '0 7 * * *');
      expect(job.state, CronState.active);
      expect(job.nextRun, isNotNull);
      expect(job.lastError, isNull);
    });

    test('a job with no name shows its prompt\'s first line', () {
      final job = CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(
        Json.of({'id': 'j2', 'prompt': ' Check the logs \nthen post', 'schedule': '30m'}),
      )!;
      expect(job.title, 'Check the logs');
      expect(job.scheduleText, '30m');
    });

    test('a job with neither name nor prompt shows its id', () {
      expect(CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(Json.of({'id': 'j3'}))!.title, 'j3');
    });

    test('a paused job and an unparseable date do not throw', () {
      final job = CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(
        Json.of({'id': 'j4', 'enabled': false, 'next_run_at': 'soon'}),
      )!;
      expect(job.state, CronState.paused);
      expect(job.nextRun, isNull);
    });

    test('a row with no id is dropped', () {
      expect(CronJob.fromJsonOrNull(Json.of({'name': 'x'})), isNull);
    });
  });

  group('JobDraft', () {
    test('create leaves unset optionals out', () {
      const draft = JobDraft(schedule: ' every 2h ', prompt: ' Do it ');
      expect(draft.toCreateBody(), {'schedule': 'every 2h', 'prompt': 'Do it'});
    });

    test('update clears a model that was reset to the default', () {
      const draft = JobDraft(schedule: '30m', prompt: 'x');
      expect(draft.toUpdateBody()['model'], isNull);
      expect(draft.toUpdateBody().containsKey('model'), isTrue);
    });
  });

  group('DashboardCronRepository', () {
    test('lists jobs for the default profile without a profile query', () async {
      http.on(
        'GET',
        '/api/cron/jobs',
        (_) => FakeReply.json({
          'jobs': [
            {'id': 'a', 'name': 'A'},
            {'name': 'no id'},
          ],
        }),
      );
      final jobs = (await repo().jobs()).valueOrNull!;
      expect(jobs.map((j) => j.id), ['a']);
      expect(http.requests.single.uri.queryParameters, isEmpty);
    });

    test('scopes a named profile in the query', () async {
      http.on('GET', '/api/cron/jobs', (_) => FakeReply.json([]));
      await repo(profile: 'research').jobs();
      expect(http.requests.single.uri.queryParameters['profile'], 'research');
    });

    test('pause and resume use their own routes', () async {
      http.on('POST', '/api/cron/jobs/a/pause',
          (_) => FakeReply.json({'id': 'a', 'enabled': false}));
      http.on('POST', '/api/cron/jobs/a/resume',
          (_) => FakeReply.json({'job': {'id': 'a', 'enabled': true}}));
      final r = repo();
      expect((await r.setEnabled('a', enabled: false)).valueOrNull!.enabled, isFalse);
      expect((await r.setEnabled('a', enabled: true)).valueOrNull!.enabled, isTrue);
    });

    test('create posts the draft and returns the job', () async {
      http.on('POST', '/api/cron/jobs',
          (_) => FakeReply.json({'id': 'new', 'name': 'N'}));
      final result = await repo().create(
        const JobDraft(schedule: '30m', prompt: 'p', name: 'N'),
      );
      expect(result.valueOrNull!.id, 'new');
      expect(http.requests.single.data, {
        'schedule': '30m',
        'prompt': 'p',
        'name': 'N',
      });
    });

    test('a server rejection keeps its message', () async {
      http.on('POST', '/api/cron/jobs', (_) =>
          FakeReply.json({'detail': "Couldn't parse 'x'"}, status: 422));
      final result = await repo().create(
        const JobDraft(schedule: 'x', prompt: 'p'),
      );
      expect(result.failureOrNull?.message, contains("Couldn't parse"));
    });

    test('runs reads sessions from a list or a runs key', () async {
      http.on('GET', '/api/cron/jobs/a/runs', (_) => FakeReply.json({
            'runs': [
              {'id': 's1', 'title': 'Run 1', 'started_at': 1760684400},
            ],
          }));
      final runs = (await repo().runs('a')).valueOrNull!;
      expect(runs.single.id, 's1');
      expect(http.requests.single.uri.queryParameters['limit'], '20');
    });

    test('an id is escaped in the path', () async {
      http.on('DELETE', '/api/cron/jobs/a%20b', (_) => FakeReply.json({'ok': true}));
      expect((await repo().delete('a b')).isOk, isTrue);
    });

    test('a missing route is a not-found failure', () async {
      expect((await repo().jobs()).failureOrNull?.kind.name, 'notFound');
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the Kanban models**

Before writing `KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull`, read the probe note's answer to "does a task carry a session link, and under which key". If it names a key other than `session_id` or `stored_session_id`, put that key in the `json.any([...])` list for `sessionId` below; if it says there is none, still write the field (it stays null and Task 7 shows no Open session button).


Create `lib/data/automation/kanban_models.dart`:

```dart
/// The Kanban plugin's board: columns, task cards and one task's detail.
library;

import '../../core/json.dart';

abstract final class KanbanColumns {
  /// Hermes sets these; a person does not move a card into them.
  static const systemSet = {'running', 'review', 'scheduled'};

  /// The columns a new task can start in.
  static const creatable = ['triage', 'todo'];

  static bool isSystemSet(String id) => systemSet.contains(id);

  static const _labels = {
    'triage': 'Triage',
    'todo': 'To do',
    'ready': 'Ready',
    'running': 'Running',
    'blocked': 'Blocked',
    'review': 'Review',
    'done': 'Done',
    'scheduled': 'Scheduled',
  };

  /// The column's name for people. An unknown id is capitalised, not hidden.
  static String label(String id) {
    final known = _labels[id];
    if (known != null) return known;
    if (id.isEmpty) return id;
    final spaced = id.replaceAll('_', ' ');
    return spaced[0].toUpperCase() + spaced.substring(1);
  }
}

final class KanbanProgress {
  const KanbanProgress(this.done, this.total);
  final int done;
  final int total;
}

final class KanbanTask {
  const KanbanTask({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.status,
    this.body,
    this.assignee,
    this.priority,
    this.latestSummary,
    this.commentCount = 0,
    this.warningCount = 0,
    this.progress,
    this.startedAt,
    this.blockKind,
    this.sessionId,
  });

  static KanbanTask? fromJsonOrNull(Json json) {
    final id = json['id'].stringOrNull;
    if (id == null || id.isEmpty) return null;
    final progress = json['progress'];
    final total = progress['total'].intOrNull;
    return KanbanTask(
      id: id,
      title: json['title'].string(id),
      status: json['status'].string(),
      body: _clean(json['body'].stringOrNull),
      assignee: _clean(json['assignee'].stringOrNull),
      priority: json['priority'].intOrNull,
      latestSummary: _clean(json['latest_summary'].stringOrNull),
      commentCount: json['comment_count'].integer(),
      warningCount: json['warnings']['count'].integer(),
      progress: total == null || total <= 0
          ? null
          : KanbanProgress(progress['done'].integer(), total),
      startedAt: json.any(['current_run_started_at', 'started_at']).dateTimeOrNull,
      blockKind: _clean(json['block_kind'].stringOrNull),
      // Task 0 records whether the wire carries a session link and under which
      // key; this reads the likeliest spellings and is null otherwise.
      sessionId: _clean(
        json.any(['session_id', 'stored_session_id']).stringOrNull,
      ),
    );
  }

  static String? _clean(String? value) {
    final trimmed = value?.trim();
    return trimmed == null || trimmed.isEmpty ? null : trimmed;
  }

  final String id;
  final String title;

  /// The column id: `triage`, `todo`, `ready`, `running`, `blocked`, `review`,
  /// `done`, or something a newer server added.
  final String status;
  final String? body;
  final String? assignee;
  final int? priority;
  final String? latestSummary;
  final int commentCount;
  final int warningCount;
  final KanbanProgress? progress;
  final DateTime? startedAt;

  /// `dependency`, `needs_input`, `capability`, `transient`, or null. Only
  /// meaningful while [status] is `blocked`.
  final String? blockKind;
  final String? sessionId;

  bool get running => status == 'running';

  KanbanTask copyWith({String? status}) => KanbanTask(
    id: id,
    title: title,
    status: status ?? this.status,
    body: body,
    assignee: assignee,
    priority: priority,
    latestSummary: latestSummary,
    commentCount: commentCount,
    warningCount: warningCount,
    progress: progress,
    startedAt: startedAt,
    blockKind: blockKind,
    sessionId: sessionId,
  );
}

final class KanbanColumn {
  const KanbanColumn({required this.id, required this.tasks});

  final String id;
  final List<KanbanTask> tasks;
  String get label => KanbanColumns.label(id);
}

final class KanbanBoard {
  const KanbanBoard({required this.columns, this.assignees = const []});

  factory KanbanBoard.fromJson(Json json) => KanbanBoard(
    columns: [
      for (final column in json['columns'].list)
        if (column['name'].stringOrNull case final id?)
          KanbanColumn(
            id: id,
            tasks: [
              for (final task in column['tasks'].list)
                ?KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull(task),
            ],
          ),
    ],
    assignees: [
      for (final name in json['assignees'].list)
        if (name.stringOrNull case final value?) value,
    ],
  );

  final List<KanbanColumn> columns;
  final List<String> assignees;

  KanbanColumn? column(String id) {
    for (final column in columns) {
      if (column.id == id) return column;
    }
    return null;
  }

  KanbanTask? task(String id) {
    for (final column in columns) {
      for (final task in column.tasks) {
        if (task.id == id) return task;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// The same board with [id] moved to [status], for an optimistic move.
  KanbanBoard moved(String id, String status) {
    final task = this.task(id);
    if (task == null) return this;
    final moved = task.copyWith(status: status);
    final next = [
      for (final column in columns)
        KanbanColumn(
          id: column.id,
          tasks: [
            for (final t in column.tasks)
              if (t.id != id) t,
            if (column.id == status) moved,
          ],
        ),
    ];
    return KanbanBoard(
      columns: next.any((c) => c.id == status)
          ? next
          : [...next, KanbanColumn(id: status, tasks: [moved])],
      assignees: assignees,
    );
  }
}

final class KanbanLink {
  const KanbanLink({required this.id, required this.title, this.status});
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String? status;
}

final class KanbanComment {
  const KanbanComment({required this.author, required this.body, this.at});
  final String author;
  final String body;
  final DateTime? at;
}

final class KanbanRun {
  const KanbanRun({this.startedAt, this.endedAt, this.outcome});
  final DateTime? startedAt;
  final DateTime? endedAt;

  /// `completed`, `failed`, or whatever the server calls it.
  final String? outcome;
}

final class KanbanTaskDetail {
  const KanbanTaskDetail({
    required this.task,
    this.result,
    this.lastFailure,
    this.comments = const [],
    this.runs = const [],
    this.parents = const [],
    this.children = const [],
  });

  factory KanbanTaskDetail.fromJson(Json json) {
    final task = KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull(json['task']);
    if (task == null) {
      throw const FormatException('No task in the detail response');
    }
    final known = <String, KanbanLink>{
      for (final row in json['link_tasks'].list)
        if (row['id'].stringOrNull case final id?)
          id: KanbanLink(
            id: id,
            title: row['title'].string(id),
            status: row['status'].stringOrNull,
          ),
    };
    List<KanbanLink> links(Json ids) => [
      for (final id in ids.list)
        if (id.stringOrNull case final value?)
          known[value] ?? KanbanLink(id: value, title: value),
    ];
    return KanbanTaskDetail(
      task: task,
      result: KanbanTask._clean(json['task']['result'].stringOrNull),
      lastFailure: KanbanTask._clean(
        json['task']['last_failure_error'].stringOrNull,
      ),
      comments: [
        for (final row in json['comments'].list)
          KanbanComment(
            author: row['author'].string('unknown'),
            body: row['body'].string(),
            at: row['created_at'].dateTimeOrNull,
          ),
      ],
      runs: [
        for (final row in json['runs'].list)
          KanbanRun(
            startedAt: row['started_at'].dateTimeOrNull,
            endedAt: row['ended_at'].dateTimeOrNull,
            outcome: KanbanTask._clean(
              row.any(['outcome', 'status']).stringOrNull,
            ),
          ),
      ],
      parents: links(json['links']['parents']),
      children: links(json['links']['children']),
    );
  }

  final KanbanTask task;
  final String? result;
  final String? lastFailure;
  final List<KanbanComment> comments;
  final List<KanbanRun> runs;
  final List<KanbanLink> parents;
  final List<KanbanLink> children;
}

/// What the New task form sends.
final class TaskDraft {
  const TaskDraft({
    required this.title,
    this.body,
    this.status = 'triage',
    this.assignee,
    this.priority,
  });

  final String title;
  final String? body;

  /// `triage` or `todo`.
  final String status;
  final String? assignee;
  final int? priority;

  Map<String, Object?> toBody() => {
    'title': title.trim(),
    if (body != null && body!.trim().isNotEmpty) 'body': body!.trim(),
    'status': status,
    if (assignee != null && assignee!.isNotEmpty) 'assignee': assignee,
    if (priority != null) 'priority': priority,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the Kanban repository**

Create `lib/data/automation/kanban_repository.dart`:

```dart
/// The Kanban plugin: the board, one task, a new task, a move.
library;

import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../net/api_client.dart';
import 'kanban_models.dart';

abstract interface class KanbanRepository {
  Future<Result<KanbanBoard>> board();
  Future<Result<KanbanTaskDetail>> task(String id);

  /// Creates a task and returns it, or null when the server did not echo it.
  Future<Result<KanbanTask?>> create(TaskDraft draft);

  /// Moves a task to [status] (a column id).
  Future<Result<void>> move(String id, String status);
}

final class DashboardKanbanRepository implements KanbanRepository {
  DashboardKanbanRepository(this._client);

  final ApiClient _client;

  static const _base = '/api/plugins/kanban';

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanBoard>> board() async =>
      (await _client.get('$_base/board')).map(KanbanBoard.fromJson);

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTaskDetail>> task(String id) async {
    final result = await _client.get('$_base/tasks/${Uri.encodeComponent(id)}');
    return result.flatMap((body) {
      try {
        return Ok(KanbanTaskDetail.fromJson(body));
      } on FormatException catch (e) {
        return Err(IrisFailure.malformed(message: e.message));
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTask?>> create(TaskDraft draft) async =>
      (await _client.post('$_base/tasks', body: draft.toBody())).map(
        (body) => KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull(body['task']),
      );

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> move(String id, String status) async =>
      (await _client.patch(
        '$_base/tasks/${Uri.encodeComponent(id)}',
        body: {'status': status},
      )).map((_) {});
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';

import '../support/fake_http.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeHttp http;
  setUp(() => http = FakeHttp());

  DashboardKanbanRepository repo() {
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      const ServerConfig(id: 's', baseUrl: 'https://h.example', label: 'S'),
      adapter: http,
    );
    addTearDown(connection.close);
    return DashboardKanbanRepository(connection.client);
  }

  final board = {
    'columns': [
      {
        'name': 'running',
        'tasks': [
          {
            'id': 't_30',
            'title': 'Investigate heartbeat gaps',
            'status': 'running',
            'assignee': 'homelab',
            'priority': 2,
            'started_at': 1760684400,
            'progress': {'done': 2, 'total': 5},
            'warnings': {'count': 1},
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        'name': 'blocked',
        'tasks': [
          {
            'id': 't_31',
            'title': 'Backup failing',
            'status': 'blocked',
            'block_kind': 'needs_input',
          },
        ],
      },
      {'name': 'sprinting', 'tasks': <Object>[]},
    ],
    'assignees': ['homelab'],
  };

  test('parses the board with an unknown column kept', () async {
    http.on('GET', '/api/plugins/kanban/board', (_) => FakeReply.json(board));
    final parsed = (await repo().board()).valueOrNull!;
    expect(parsed.columns.map((c) => c.id), ['running', 'blocked', 'sprinting']);
    expect(parsed.columns.last.label, 'Sprinting');
    final task = parsed.task('t_30')!;
    expect(task.priority, 2);
    expect(task.progress!.done, 2);
    expect(task.progress!.total, 5);
    expect(task.startedAt, isNotNull);
    expect(task.warningCount, 1);
    expect(parsed.task('t_31')!.blockKind, 'needs_input');
  });

  test('a task with no optional fields still parses', () {
    final task = KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull(
      Json.of({'id': 't_1', 'title': 'x', 'status': 'todo'}),
    )!;
    expect(task.progress, isNull);
    expect(task.startedAt, isNull);
    expect(task.commentCount, 0);
    expect(task.sessionId, isNull);
  });

  test('progress with a zero total is dropped', () {
    final task = KanbanTask.fromJsonOrNull(Json.of({
      'id': 't_1',
      'title': 'x',
      'status': 'running',
      'progress': {'done': 0, 'total': 0},
    }))!;
    expect(task.progress, isNull);
  });

  test('system-set columns are locked', () {
    expect(KanbanColumns.isSystemSet('running'), isTrue);
    expect(KanbanColumns.isSystemSet('review'), isTrue);
    expect(KanbanColumns.isSystemSet('scheduled'), isTrue);
    expect(KanbanColumns.isSystemSet('todo'), isFalse);
    expect(KanbanColumns.label('todo'), 'To do');
  });

  test('moved takes a card out of one column and into another', () {
    final parsed = KanbanBoard.fromJson(Json.of(board));
    final next = parsed.moved('t_31', 'todo');
    expect(next.column('blocked')!.tasks, isEmpty);
    expect(next.column('todo')!.tasks.single.id, 't_31');
    expect(next.task('t_31')!.status, 'todo');
  });

  test('detail joins links to their titles and keeps unknown ones', () async {
    http.on('GET', '/api/plugins/kanban/tasks/t_26', (_) => FakeReply.json({
          'task': {
            'id': 't_26',
            'title': 'Rotate keys',
            'status': 'review',
            'result': 'Keys are live.',
            'last_failure_error': 'health check timed out',
          },
          'comments': [
            {'author': 'you', 'body': 'Hold off', 'created_at': 1760684400},
          ],
          'runs': [
            {'started_at': 1760684400, 'ended_at': 1760685400, 'outcome': 'completed'},
            {'started_at': 1760680000, 'status': 'failed'},
          ],
          'links': {
            'parents': ['t_20'],
            'children': ['t_34', 't_99'],
          },
          'link_tasks': [
            {'id': 't_20', 'title': 'Q4 hygiene', 'status': 'todo'},
            {'id': 't_34', 'title': 'Update api-edge', 'status': 'done'},
          ],
        }));
    final detail = (await repo().task('t_26')).valueOrNull!;
    expect(detail.result, 'Keys are live.');
    expect(detail.lastFailure, 'health check timed out');
    expect(detail.parents.single.title, 'Q4 hygiene');
    expect(detail.children.map((c) => c.title), ['Update api-edge', 't_99']);
    expect(detail.runs.map((r) => r.outcome), ['completed', 'failed']);
    expect(detail.comments.single.author, 'you');
  });

  test('a detail response with no task is malformed, not a crash', () async {
    http.on('GET', '/api/plugins/kanban/tasks/x', (_) => FakeReply.json({}));
    expect((await repo().task('x')).failureOrNull?.kind.name, 'malformed');
  });

  test('create posts the draft; move patches the status', () async {
    http.on('POST', '/api/plugins/kanban/tasks', (_) => FakeReply.json({
          'task': {'id': 't_40', 'title': 'New', 'status': 'triage'},
        }));
    http.on('PATCH', '/api/plugins/kanban/tasks/t_40', (_) => FakeReply.json({}));
    final r = repo();
    final created = (await r.create(
      const TaskDraft(title: ' New ', body: '', priority: 2),
    )).valueOrNull;
    expect(created!.id, 't_40');
    expect(http.requests.first.data, {
      'title': 'New',
      'status': 'triage',
      'priority': 2,
    });
    expect((await r.move('t_40', 'todo')).isOk, isTrue);
    expect(http.requests.last.data, {'status': 'todo'});
  });

  test('the board parameter is never sent', () async {
    http.on('GET', '/api/plugins/kanban/board', (_) => FakeReply.json(board));
    await repo().board();
    expect(http.requests.single.uri.queryParameters.containsKey('board'), isFalse);
  });
}
```

```dart
/// Profile management: create, rename, edit the SOUL, delete.
///
/// Listing stays on `ChatBackend.profiles()` and `profilesProvider`, which
/// already read `GET /api/profiles`.
library;

import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../net/api_client.dart';

/// What a new profile starts from.
enum ProfileStart { blank, copy, full }

abstract final class ProfileName {
  static final _valid = RegExp(r'^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$');

  /// An error to show under the field, or null when [name] is acceptable.
  /// An empty name is not an error: nothing has been typed yet.
  static String? problem(String name) {
    if (name.isEmpty) return null;
    if (_valid.hasMatch(name)) return null;
    return 'Lowercase letters, digits, - and _ only';
  }

  static bool isValid(String name) => _valid.hasMatch(name);
}

final class ProfileDraft {
  const ProfileDraft({
    required this.name,
    this.start = ProfileStart.blank,
    this.source = 'default',
    this.soul,
  });

  final String name;
  final ProfileStart start;

  /// The profile copied from when [start] is not blank.
  final String source;
  final String? soul;

  Map<String, Object?> toBody() => {
    'name': name,
    if (soul != null && soul!.trim().isNotEmpty) 'soul': soul!.trim(),
    if (start != ProfileStart.blank)
      if (source == 'default')
        'clone_from_default': true
      else
        'clone_from': source,
    if (start == ProfileStart.full) 'clone_all': true,
  };
}

abstract interface class ProfileRepository {
  Future<Result<void>> create(ProfileDraft draft);
  Future<Result<void>> rename(String name, String newName);
  Future<Result<String>> soul(String name);
  Future<Result<void>> setSoul(String name, String content);
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String name);
}

final class DashboardProfileRepository implements ProfileRepository {
  DashboardProfileRepository(this._client);

  final ApiClient _client;

  /// Creating a profile copies skills, which can take a while.
  static const createTimeout = Duration(seconds: 90);

  static String _path(String name, [String suffix = '']) =>
      '/api/profiles/${Uri.encodeComponent(name)}$suffix';

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> create(ProfileDraft draft) async => (await _client.post(
    '/api/profiles',
    body: draft.toBody(),
    receiveTimeout: createTimeout,
  )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> rename(String name, String newName) async =>
      (await _client.patch(_path(name), body: {'new_name': newName})).map(
        (_) {},
      );

  @override
  Future<Result<String>> soul(String name) async =>
      (await _client.get(_path(name, '/soul'))).map(
        (body) => body['content'].string(),
      );

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setSoul(String name, String content) async =>
      (await _client.put(_path(name, '/soul'), body: {'content': content}))
          .map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String name) async =>
      (await _client.delete(_path(name))).map((_) {});
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';

import '../support/fake_http.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeHttp http;
  setUp(() => http = FakeHttp());

  DashboardProfileRepository repo() {
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      const ServerConfig(id: 's', baseUrl: 'https://h.example', label: 'S'),
      adapter: http,
    );
    addTearDown(connection.close);
    return DashboardProfileRepository(connection.client);
  }

  group('ProfileName', () {
    test('accepts lowercase letters, digits, - and _', () {
      for (final name in ['research', 'work-notes', 'a1_b2', 'x']) {
        expect(ProfileName.isValid(name), isTrue, reason: name);
        expect(ProfileName.problem(name), isNull);
      }
    });

    test('rejects capitals, spaces and a leading dash', () {
      for (final name in ['Work Notes', 'Research', '-x', 'a b', 'é']) {
        expect(ProfileName.isValid(name), isFalse, reason: name);
        expect(ProfileName.problem(name), isNotNull);
      }
    });

    test('an empty name is not an error yet, but is not valid', () {
      expect(ProfileName.problem(''), isNull);
      expect(ProfileName.isValid(''), isFalse);
    });
  });

  group('ProfileDraft.toBody', () {
    test('blank sends the name and the SOUL only', () {
      expect(const ProfileDraft(name: 'a', soul: ' hi ').toBody(), {
        'name': 'a',
        'soul': 'hi',
      });
    });

    test('copy of default uses clone_from_default', () {
      expect(
        const ProfileDraft(name: 'a', start: ProfileStart.copy).toBody(),
        {'name': 'a', 'clone_from_default': true},
      );
    });

    test('full copy of another profile uses clone_from and clone_all', () {
      expect(
        const ProfileDraft(
          name: 'a',
          start: ProfileStart.full,
          source: 'research',
        ).toBody(),
        {'name': 'a', 'clone_from': 'research', 'clone_all': true},
      );
    });
  });

  group('DashboardProfileRepository', () {
    test('rename patches new_name', () async {
      http.on('PATCH', '/api/profiles/old', (_) => const FakeReply(200, body: '{}'));
      expect((await repo().rename('old', 'new')).isOk, isTrue);
      expect(http.requests.single.data, {'new_name': 'new'});
    });

    test('soul reads content and setSoul writes it', () async {
      http.on('GET', '/api/profiles/a/soul',
          (_) => const FakeReply(200, body: '{"content":"# A","exists":true}'));
      http.on('PUT', '/api/profiles/a/soul', (_) => const FakeReply(200, body: '{"ok":true}'));
      final r = repo();
      expect((await r.soul('a')).valueOrNull, '# A');
      expect((await r.setSoul('a', '# B')).isOk, isTrue);
      expect(http.requests.last.data, {'content': '# B'});
    });

    test('delete uses DELETE and a refusal keeps its message', () async {
      http.on('DELETE', '/api/profiles/default', (_) => const FakeReply(
            400,
            body: '{"detail":"The default profile cannot be deleted."}',
          ));
      final result = await repo().delete('default');
      expect(result.failureOrNull?.message, contains('cannot be deleted'));
    });
  });
}
```

### Task 2: Providers, polling and test fakes

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/app/automation_providers.dart`
- Create: `lib/features/automation_errors.dart`
- Create: `test/support/fake_automation.dart`
- Test: `test/app/automation_providers_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's repositories; `activeSessionProvider`, `activeProfileProvider`, `activeCapabilitiesProvider`, `liveIntervalProvider`, `demoModeProvider` from `lib/app/providers.dart` and `lib/demo/demo_mode.dart`; `session.connection.client`.
- Produces:
  - `enum HomeTab { sessions, scheduled, tasks }`
  - `homeTabProvider` (`NotifierProvider<HomeTabState, HomeTab>`, `.notifier.select(HomeTab)`), `homeTabsProvider` (`Provider<List<HomeTab>>`, empty when no extra tab is confirmed), `visibleHomeTabProvider` (`Provider<HomeTab>`)
  - `cronRepositoryProvider` (`FutureProvider<CronRepository?>`), `kanbanRepositoryProvider` (`FutureProvider<KanbanRepository?>`), `profileRepositoryProvider` (`FutureProvider<ProfileRepository?>`)
  - `cronJobsProvider` (`StreamProvider.autoDispose<List<CronJob>>`), `kanbanBoardProvider` (`StreamProvider.autoDispose<KanbanBoard>`)
  - `cronRunsProvider` (`FutureProvider.autoDispose.family<List<ConversationSummary>, String>`), `deliveryTargetsProvider` (`FutureProvider.autoDispose<List<DeliveryTarget>>`, empty on failure)
  - `cronEnabledOverridesProvider` (`NotifierProvider<CronEnabledOverrides, Map<String, bool>>` with `set(id, enabled)` and `clear(id)`), `taskMoveOverridesProvider` (`NotifierProvider<TaskMoveOverrides, Map<String, String>>` with `set(id, status)` and `clear(id)`)
  - `String describeWrite(IrisFailure)` in `lib/features/automation_errors.dart`
  - `FakeCronRepository`, `FakeKanbanRepository`, `FakeProfileRepository` in `test/support/fake_automation.dart`

```dart
/// Scripted repositories, so the tabs and forms are tested without HTTP.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:iris/core/failure.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';

CronJob fakeJob(
  String id, {
  String? name,
  String? prompt,
  String schedule = '0 7 * * *',
  String? display,
  bool enabled = true,
  CronState state = CronState.active,
  DateTime? nextRun,
  DateTime? lastRun,
  String? lastError,
  String? model,
  String? deliver,
}) => CronJob(
  id: id,
  name: name,
  prompt: prompt,
  schedule: schedule,
  scheduleDisplay: display,
  enabled: enabled,
  state: state,
  nextRun: nextRun,
  lastRun: lastRun,
  lastError: lastError,
  model: model,
  deliver: deliver,
);

KanbanTask fakeTask(
  String id, {
  String? title,
  String status = 'todo',
  String? assignee,
  int? priority,
  String? summary,
  KanbanProgress? progress,
  DateTime? startedAt,
  String? blockKind,
  int comments = 0,
  int warnings = 0,
  String? sessionId,
}) => KanbanTask(
  id: id,
  title: title ?? 'Task $id',
  status: status,
  assignee: assignee,
  priority: priority,
  latestSummary: summary,
  progress: progress,
  startedAt: startedAt,
  blockKind: blockKind,
  commentCount: comments,
  warningCount: warnings,
  sessionId: sessionId,
);

final class FakeCronRepository implements CronRepository {
  Result<List<CronJob>> jobsResult = const Ok([]);
  Result<List<ConversationSummary>> runsResult = const Ok([]);
  Result<List<DeliveryTarget>> targetsResult = const Ok([]);
  Result<CronJob>? writeResult;
  Result<void> voidResult = const Ok(null);

  /// When set, `setEnabled` waits for it, so a test can look at the screen
  /// between the tap and the server's answer.
  Completer<void>? setEnabledGate;

  /// `jobs`, `setEnabled <id> <bool>`, `trigger <id>`, `delete <id>`,
  /// `create`, `update <id>`, `runs <id>`, `targets`.
  final List<String> calls = [];
  final List<JobDraft> drafts = [];

  Result<CronJob> get _write =>
      writeResult ??
      Ok(
        CronJob(
          id: 'new',
          schedule: '30m',
          enabled: true,
          state: CronState.active,
        ),
      );

  @override
  Future<Result<List<CronJob>>> jobs() async {
    calls.add('jobs');
    return jobsResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> job(String id) async {
    calls.add('job $id');
    return _write;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<List<ConversationSummary>>> runs(
    String id, {
    int limit = 20,
  }) async {
    calls.add('runs $id');
    return runsResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<List<DeliveryTarget>>> deliveryTargets() async {
    calls.add('targets');
    return targetsResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> create(JobDraft draft) async {
    calls.add('create');
    drafts.add(draft);
    return _write;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> update(String id, JobDraft draft) async {
    calls.add('update $id');
    drafts.add(draft);
    return _write;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> setEnabled(
    String id, {
    required bool enabled,
  }) async {
    calls.add('setEnabled $id $enabled');
    await setEnabledGate?.future;
    return _write;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> trigger(String id) async {
    calls.add('trigger $id');
    return voidResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String id) async {
    calls.add('delete $id');
    return voidResult;
  }
}

final class FakeKanbanRepository implements KanbanRepository {
  Result<KanbanBoard> boardResult = const Ok(KanbanBoard(columns: []));
  Result<KanbanTaskDetail>? detailResult;
  Result<KanbanTask?> createResult = const Ok(null);
  Result<void> moveResult = const Ok(null);

  /// `board`, `task <id>`, `create`, `move <id> <status>`.
  final List<String> calls = [];
  final List<TaskDraft> drafts = [];

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanBoard>> board() async {
    calls.add('board');
    return boardResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTaskDetail>> task(String id) async {
    calls.add('task $id');
    return detailResult ??
        Err(const IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound, message: 'no detail'));
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTask?>> create(TaskDraft draft) async {
    calls.add('create');
    drafts.add(draft);
    return createResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> move(String id, String status) async {
    calls.add('move $id $status');
    return moveResult;
  }
}

final class FakeProfileRepository implements ProfileRepository {
  Result<void> createResult = const Ok(null);
  Result<void> renameResult = const Ok(null);
  Result<String> soulResult = const Ok('');
  Result<void> setSoulResult = const Ok(null);
  Result<void> deleteResult = const Ok(null);

  /// `create <name>`, `rename <old> <new>`, `soul <name>`,
  /// `setSoul <name>`, `delete <name>`.
  final List<String> calls = [];
  final List<ProfileDraft> drafts = [];
  final Map<String, String> writtenSouls = {};

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> create(ProfileDraft draft) async {
    calls.add('create ${draft.name}');
    drafts.add(draft);
    return createResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> rename(String name, String newName) async {
    calls.add('rename $name $newName');
    return renameResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<String>> soul(String name) async {
    calls.add('soul $name');
    return soulResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setSoul(String name, String content) async {
    calls.add('setSoul $name');
    writtenSouls[name] = content;
    return setSoulResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String name) async {
    calls.add('delete $name');
    return deleteResult;
  }
}
```

Create `test/app/automation_providers_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/automation_providers.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/providers.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/failure.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/capabilities.dart';

import '../support/fake_automation.dart';

Future<void> until(bool Function() ok) async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 200 && !ok(); i++) {
    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 10));
  }
  expect(ok(), isTrue, reason: 'timed out waiting');
}

ServerCapabilities probed(Set<ServerCapability> available) =>
    ServerCapabilities(available: available, probedAt: DateTime(2026, 10, 17));

void main() {
  TestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  group('home tabs', () {
    Future<ProviderContainer> container(ServerCapabilities caps) async {
      final c = ProviderContainer(
        overrides: [
          activeCapabilitiesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value(caps)),
        ],
      );
      addTearDown(c.dispose);
      c.listen(activeCapabilitiesProvider, (_, _) {});
      await c.read(activeCapabilitiesProvider.future);
      return c;
    }

    test('an unprobed server offers no extra tabs', () async {
      final c = await container(const ServerCapabilities.unknown());
      expect(c.read(homeTabsProvider), isEmpty);
    });

    test('a server with neither capability offers none', () async {
      final c = await container(probed({ServerCapability.profiles}));
      expect(c.read(homeTabsProvider), isEmpty);
      expect(c.read(visibleHomeTabProvider), HomeTab.sessions);
    });

    test('cron only offers Sessions and Scheduled', () async {
      final c = await container(probed({ServerCapability.cronJobs}));
      expect(c.read(homeTabsProvider), [HomeTab.sessions, HomeTab.scheduled]);
    });

    test('both offer three, and a chosen tab sticks', () async {
      final c = await container(
        probed({ServerCapability.cronJobs, ServerCapability.kanban}),
      );
      expect(c.read(homeTabsProvider), HomeTab.values);
      c.read(homeTabProvider.notifier).select(HomeTab.tasks);
      expect(c.read(visibleHomeTabProvider), HomeTab.tasks);
    });

    test('a chosen tab the server lacks falls back to Sessions', () async {
      final c = await container(probed({ServerCapability.cronJobs}));
      c.read(homeTabProvider.notifier).select(HomeTab.tasks);
      expect(c.read(visibleHomeTabProvider), HomeTab.sessions);
    });
  });

  group('polling', () {
    test('keeps the last good list when a poll fails', () async {
      final cron = FakeCronRepository()..jobsResult = Ok([fakeJob('a')]);
      final c = ProviderContainer(
        overrides: [
          cronRepositoryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => cron),
          liveIntervalProvider.overrideWithValue(
            const Duration(milliseconds: 20),
          ),
        ],
      );
      addTearDown(c.dispose);
      c.listen(cronJobsProvider, (_, _) {});
      await until(() => c.read(cronJobsProvider).value?.length == 1);

      cron.jobsResult = const Err(IrisFailure.offline());
      await until(() => c.read(cronJobsProvider).hasError);
      expect(c.read(cronJobsProvider).value, hasLength(1));

      cron.jobsResult = Ok([fakeJob('a'), fakeJob('b')]);
      await until(() => c.read(cronJobsProvider).value?.length == 2);
      expect(c.read(cronJobsProvider).hasError, isFalse);
    });

    test('stops polling once nothing listens', () async {
      final cron = FakeCronRepository()..jobsResult = Ok([fakeJob('a')]);
      final c = ProviderContainer(
        overrides: [
          cronRepositoryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => cron),
          liveIntervalProvider.overrideWithValue(
            const Duration(milliseconds: 20),
          ),
        ],
      );
      addTearDown(c.dispose);
      final sub = c.listen(cronJobsProvider, (_, _) {});
      await until(() => cron.calls.length >= 2);
      sub.close();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
      final settled = cron.calls.length;
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
      expect(cron.calls.length, settled);
    });

    test('no repository means an empty list, not an error', () async {
      final c = ProviderContainer(
        overrides: [cronRepositoryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null)],
      );
      addTearDown(c.dispose);
      c.listen(cronJobsProvider, (_, _) {});
      await until(() => c.read(cronJobsProvider).hasValue);
      expect(c.read(cronJobsProvider).value, isEmpty);
    });
  });

  group('overrides', () {
    test('set and clear', () {
      final c = ProviderContainer();
      addTearDown(c.dispose);
      c.read(cronEnabledOverridesProvider.notifier).set('a', false);
      expect(c.read(cronEnabledOverridesProvider), {'a': false});
      c.read(cronEnabledOverridesProvider.notifier).clear('a');
      expect(c.read(cronEnabledOverridesProvider), isEmpty);
      c.read(taskMoveOverridesProvider.notifier).set('t', 'todo');
      expect(c.read(taskMoveOverridesProvider), {'t': 'todo'});
    });
  });

  test('delivery targets fall back to empty on a failure', () async {
    final cron = FakeCronRepository()
      ..targetsResult = const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    final c = ProviderContainer(
      overrides: [cronRepositoryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => cron)],
    );
    addTearDown(c.dispose);
    c.listen(deliveryTargetsProvider, (_, _) {});
    expect(await c.read(deliveryTargetsProvider.future), isEmpty);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the providers**

Create `lib/app/automation_providers.dart`:

```dart
/// What home's extra tabs and the profile tools read: the repositories, the
/// polled lists, and the optimistic overrides.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../core/result.dart';
import '../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../data/automation/cron_repository.dart';
import '../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../data/automation/kanban_repository.dart';
import '../data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import '../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../data/net/capabilities.dart';
import '../demo/demo_mode.dart';
import 'providers.dart';

/// The tabs on home.
enum HomeTab {
  sessions('Sessions'),
  scheduled('Scheduled'),
  tasks('Tasks');

  const HomeTab(this.label);
  final String label;
}

/// The tab the person picked. Lasts for the app session; a cold start opens on
/// Sessions.
final homeTabProvider = NotifierProvider<HomeTabState, HomeTab>(
  HomeTabState.new,
);

class HomeTabState extends Notifier<HomeTab> {
  @override
  HomeTab build() => HomeTab.sessions;

  void select(HomeTab tab) => state = tab;
}

/// The tabs this server offers. Empty unless the probe confirmed at least one
/// extra capability: a server that has neither Scheduled nor Tasks gets the
/// home it has always had, with no control drawn.
final homeTabsProvider = Provider<List<HomeTab>>((ref) {
  final capabilities =
      ref.watch(activeCapabilitiesProvider).value ??
      const ServerCapabilities.unknown();
  final extra = [
    if (capabilities.confirmed(ServerCapability.cronJobs)) HomeTab.scheduled,
    if (capabilities.confirmed(ServerCapability.kanban)) HomeTab.tasks,
  ];
  return extra.isEmpty ? const [] : [HomeTab.sessions, ...extra];
});

/// The picked tab, or Sessions when the server does not offer it.
final visibleHomeTabProvider = Provider<HomeTab>((ref) {
  final tabs = ref.watch(homeTabsProvider);
  final picked = ref.watch(homeTabProvider);
  return tabs.contains(picked) ? picked : HomeTab.sessions;
});

/// Cron jobs for the active profile. Null until the link is live, and in the
/// demo until Task 13 gives it a repository.
final cronRepositoryProvider = FutureProvider<CronRepository?>((ref) async {
  if (ref.watch(demoModeProvider)) return null;
  final session = await ref.watch(activeSessionProvider.future);
  if (session == null) return null;
  return DashboardCronRepository(
    session.connection.client,
    profile: ref.watch(activeProfileProvider),
  );
});

final kanbanRepositoryProvider = FutureProvider<KanbanRepository?>((ref) async {
  if (ref.watch(demoModeProvider)) return null;
  final session = await ref.watch(activeSessionProvider.future);
  if (session == null) return null;
  return DashboardKanbanRepository(session.connection.client);
});

final profileRepositoryProvider = FutureProvider<ProfileRepository?>((
  ref,
) async {
  if (ref.watch(demoModeProvider)) return null;
  final session = await ref.watch(activeSessionProvider.future);
  if (session == null) return null;
  return DashboardProfileRepository(session.connection.client);
});

/// Asks [fetch] now and every `liveIntervalProvider` after, until the provider
/// is disposed. A failed poll is reported as a stream error and the next poll
/// still runs, so a list that was on screen stays on screen. Nothing is asked
/// while the app is in the background.
Stream<T> pollEvery<T>(Ref ref, Future<Result<T>> Function() fetch) {
  final interval = ref.watch(liveIntervalProvider);
  final controller = StreamController<T>();
  Timer? next;
  var disposed = false;

  Future<void> tick() async {
    if (disposed) return;
    final state = WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState;
    final background =
        state == AppLifecycleState.paused ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.hidden ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.detached;
    if (!background) {
      final result = await fetch();
      if (disposed) return;
      switch (result) {
        case Ok(:final value):
          controller.add(value);
        case Err(:final failure):
          controller.addError(failure);
      }
    }
    if (!disposed) next = Timer(interval, () => unawaited(tick()));
  }

  ref.onDispose(() {
    disposed = true;
    next?.cancel();
    unawaited(controller.close());
  });
  unawaited(tick());
  return controller.stream;
}

/// The active profile's jobs, polled while a screen watches this.
final cronJobsProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose<List<CronJob>>((
  ref,
) async* {
  final repository = await ref.watch(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
  if (repository == null) {
    yield const [];
    return;
  }
  yield* pollEvery(ref, repository.jobs);
});

/// The server's current Kanban board, polled while a screen watches this.
final kanbanBoardProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose<KanbanBoard>((
  ref,
) async* {
  final repository = await ref.watch(kanbanRepositoryProvider.future);
  if (repository == null) {
    yield const KanbanBoard(columns: []);
    return;
  }
  yield* pollEvery(ref, repository.board);
});

/// One job's past runs, newest first.
final cronRunsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<List<ConversationSummary>, String>((ref, jobId) async {
      final repository = await ref.watch(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
      if (repository == null) return const [];
      return switch (await repository.runs(jobId)) {
        Ok(:final value) => value,
        Err(:final failure) => throw failure,
      };
    });

/// Where a job's reply can be sent. A server that cannot say has none, and the
/// form hides the row.
final deliveryTargetsProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose<List<DeliveryTarget>>((
  ref,
) async {
  final repository = await ref.watch(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
  if (repository == null) return const [];
  return (await repository.deliveryTargets()).or(const []);
});

/// A pause or resume that is on screen before the server has answered.
final cronEnabledOverridesProvider =
    NotifierProvider<CronEnabledOverrides, Map<String, bool>>(
      CronEnabledOverrides.new,
    );

class CronEnabledOverrides extends Notifier<Map<String, bool>> {
  @override
  Map<String, bool> build() => const {};

  void set(String id, bool enabled) => state = {...state, id: enabled};

  void clear(String id) => state = {...state}..remove(id);
}

/// A move that is on screen before the server has answered.
final taskMoveOverridesProvider =
    NotifierProvider<TaskMoveOverrides, Map<String, String>>(
      TaskMoveOverrides.new,
    );

class TaskMoveOverrides extends Notifier<Map<String, String>> {
  @override
  Map<String, String> build() => const {};

  void set(String id, String status) => state = {...state, id: status};

  void clear(String id) => state = {...state}..remove(id);
}
```

```dart
/// How a failed write to the cron, Kanban or profile routes is worded.
library;

import '../core/failure.dart';
import 'chat/chat_controller.dart';

/// A route the server lacks is not an error in the person's request.
String describeWrite(IrisFailure failure) =>
    failure.kind == FailureKind.notFound
    ? 'Not supported on this server'
    : ChatController.describe(failure);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the component**

Create `lib/design/components/segmented.dart`:

```dart
/// A row of equal segments with a thumb that slides to the selected one.
library;

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

import '../theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../tokens/motion.dart';

/// Home's Sessions | Scheduled | Tasks, the model sheet's effort track, and the
/// New task column choice.
///
/// At large text the segments wrap onto rows and the selected one is filled in
/// place, because a thumb cannot slide across a layout that has no single row.
class IrisSegmented<T> extends StatelessWidget {
  const IrisSegmented({
    required this.values,
    required this.selected,
    required this.labelOf,
    required this.onChanged,
    this.height = 44,
    super.key,
  });

  final List<T> values;
  final T? selected;
  final String Function(T value) labelOf;
  final ValueChanged<T> onChanged;

  /// The least height of a segment.
  final double height;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final reduced = context.reduceMotion;
    final wide = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(1) > 1.4;
    final index = values.indexWhere((value) => value == selected);

    final thumb = BoxDecoration(
      color: palette.surfaceRaised,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
      border: Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
    );

    Widget segment(T value) {
      final on = value == selected;
      final label = labelOf(value);
      return Semantics(
        button: true,
        selected: on,
        label: '$label${on ? ', selected' : ''}',
        excludeSemantics: true,
        child: GestureDetector(
          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
          onTap: () => onChanged(value),
          child: Container(
            constraints: BoxConstraints(minHeight: height, minWidth: 44),
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
            decoration: wide && on ? thumb : null,
            child: Text(
              label,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: type.body.copyWith(
                fontSize: 13.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                color: on ? palette.ink : palette.inkMuted,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    final Widget content;
    if (wide) {
      content = Wrap(
        spacing: 2,
        runSpacing: 2,
        children: [for (final value in values) segment(value)],
      );
    } else {
      final count = values.length;
      content = Stack(
        children: [
          if (index >= 0)
            Positioned.fill(
              child: AnimatedAlign(
                duration: reduced ? Duration.zero : IrisDurations.normal,
                curve: IrisCurves.standard,
                alignment: Alignment(
                  count == 1 ? 0 : -1 + 2 * index / (count - 1),
                  0,
                ),
                child: FractionallySizedBox(
                  widthFactor: 1 / count,
                  heightFactor: 1,
                  child: DecoratedBox(
                    key: const Key('segmented-thumb'),
                    decoration: thumb,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          Row(
            children: [
              for (final value in values) Expanded(child: segment(value)),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      );
    }

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surfaceSunken,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: palette.line, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
      ),
      child: content,
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
```
