```dart
    );
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Schedule'), 'every blursday');
    await tester.enterText(field('Prompt'), 'x');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text("Couldn't parse 'every blursday'"), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(JobFormScreen), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('Deliver to is hidden when the server has no targets', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Deliver to'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('Deliver to shows when the server lists targets', (tester) async {
    cron.targetsResult = const Ok([
      DeliveryTarget(id: 'telegram', name: 'telegram-ops'),
    ]);
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Deliver to'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Deliver to'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('telegram-ops'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Schedule'), '30m');
    await tester.enterText(field('Prompt'), 'x');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(cron.drafts.single.deliver, 'telegram');
  });

  testWidgets('editing starts from the job and calls update', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      form(
        job: fakeJob(
          'j1',
          name: 'Digest',
          prompt: 'Summarise',
          schedule: '0 7 * * *',
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Edit job'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Digest'), findsWidgets);
    await tester.enterText(field('Prompt'), 'Summarise differently');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(cron.calls, contains('update j1'));
  });

  testWidgets('cancelling with edits asks before discarding', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Prompt'), 'half a thought');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Cancel'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Discard changes?'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Keep editing'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byType(JobFormScreen), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('cancelling a clean form just closes it', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Cancel'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Discard changes?'), findsNothing);
  });
}
```

`themed()` puts the screen as `home`, so "closes it" means the route pops to nothing; if `pumpAndSettle` throws because popping the only route leaves a black screen, wrap the form in a `Navigator`-pushing host: open it from a button with `Navigator.push(IrisPageRoute(...))` and assert `find.byType(JobFormScreen)` is gone. - [ ] **Step 4: Write the screen**

Create `lib/features/scheduled/job_form_screen.dart`:

```dart
/// A new or edited cron job (frames A5, A5·E).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/iris_scaffold.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';

class JobFormScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const JobFormScreen({this.job, super.key});

  /// The job being edited; null creates one.
  final CronJob? job;

  @override
  ConsumerState<JobFormScreen> createState() => _JobFormScreenState();
}

class _JobFormScreenState extends ConsumerState<JobFormScreen> {
  static const scheduleHint =
      '30m · every 2h · every monday 9am · 0 9 * * * · or a date';

  late final _name = TextEditingController(text: widget.job?.name ?? '');
  late final _schedule = TextEditingController(
    text: widget.job == null
        ? ''
        : (widget.job!.schedule.isNotEmpty
              ? widget.job!.schedule
              : widget.job!.scheduleText),
  );
  late final _prompt = TextEditingController(text: widget.job?.prompt ?? '');
  final _scheduleFocus = FocusNode();

  late String? _model = widget.job?.model;
  late String? _provider = widget.job?.provider;
  late String? _deliver = widget.job?.deliver;
  String? _scheduleError;
  bool _saving = false;

  late final String _initial = _signature();

  String _signature() =>
      '${_name.text}|${_schedule.text}|${_prompt.text}|$_model|$_provider|$_deliver';

  bool get _dirty => _signature() != _initial;

  bool get _canSave =>
      !_saving &&
      _schedule.text.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      _prompt.text.trim().isNotEmpty;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    for (final controller in [_name, _schedule, _prompt]) {
      controller.addListener(() {
        if (!mounted) return;
        setState(() {
          // Typing again clears what the server said about the old text.
          if (controller == _schedule) _scheduleError = null;
        });
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _name.dispose();
    _schedule.dispose();
    _prompt.dispose();
    _scheduleFocus.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _cancel() async {
    if (!_dirty) {
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
      return;
    }
    final discard = await showIrisSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
        title: 'Discard changes?',
        subtitle: 'What you typed here will be lost.',
        actions: IrisSheetActions(
          secondary: IrisButton(
            label: 'Keep editing',
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(false),
          ),
          primary: IrisButton(
            label: 'Discard',
            tone: IrisButtonTone.danger,
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(true),
          ),
        ),
        child: const SizedBox.shrink(),
      ),
    );
    if (discard == true && mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final repository = await ref.read(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
    if (repository == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _saving = true;
      _scheduleError = null;
    });
    final draft = JobDraft(
      name: _name.text,
      schedule: _schedule.text,
      prompt: _prompt.text,
      model: _model,
      provider: _provider,
      deliver: _deliver,
    );
    final existing = widget.job;
    final result = existing == null
        ? await repository.create(draft)
        : await repository.update(existing.id, draft);
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok(:final value):
        Navigator.of(context).pop(value);
      case Err(:final failure):
        setState(() => _saving = false);
        if (failure.kind == FailureKind.rejected && failure.message != null) {
          // Hermes says which part it could not read; the schedule is where
          // that nearly always is, so the message sits under it.
          setState(() => _scheduleError = failure.message);
          _scheduleFocus.requestFocus();
        } else {
          showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
        }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickModel() async {
    final chat = await ref.read(activeChatProvider.future);
    if (chat == null || !mounted) return;
    final options = (await chat.modelOptions()).valueOrNull;
    if (options == null || !mounted) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: "Couldn't load the model list");
      return;
    }
    final choice = await showIrisSheet<_ModelChoice>(
      context: context,
      builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
        title: 'Model',
        subtitle: 'Used for every run of this job.',
        child: Column(
          children: [
            IrisListRow(
              title: 'Profile default',
              selected: _model == null,
              onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(
                const _ModelChoice(null, null),
              ),
            ),
            for (final provider in options.usable) ...[
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.md),
                child: Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: IrisLabel(provider.name),
                ),
              ),
              for (final model in provider.models)
                IrisListRow(
                  title: model,
                  selected: _model == model && _provider == provider.slug,
                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(
                    _ModelChoice(model, provider.slug),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _model = choice.model;
      _provider = choice.provider;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _pickDeliver(List<DeliveryTarget> targets) async {
    final choice = await showIrisSheet<_DeliverChoice>(
      context: context,
      builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
        title: 'Deliver to',
        subtitle: "Where each run's reply is sent.",
        child: Column(
          children: [
            IrisListRow(
              title: 'Nowhere',
              selected: _deliver == null,
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(const _DeliverChoice(null)),
            ),
            for (final target in targets)
              IrisListRow(
                title: target.name,
                selected: _deliver == target.id,
                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(
                  _DeliverChoice(target.id),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (choice == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _deliver = choice.id);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final targets = ref.watch(deliveryTargetsProvider).value ?? const [];
    final deliverName = targets
        .where((t) => t.id == _deliver)
        .map((t) => t.name)
        .firstOrNull;
    return IrisScaffold(
      onBack: _cancel,
      header: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.sm, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Cancel',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
              onPressed: () => unawaited(_cancel()),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                widget.job == null ? 'New job' : 'Edit job',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: type.heading.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
              ),
            ),
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Save',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
              busy: _saving,
              onPressed: _canSave ? () => unawaited(_save()) : null,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.lg, gutter, IrisSpace.xxl),
        children: [
          IrisField(
            label: 'Name · optional',
            hint: 'Weekly planning digest',
            controller: _name,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisField(
            label: 'Schedule',
            hint: 'every monday 9am',
            controller: _schedule,
            focusNode: _scheduleFocus,
            mono: true,
            autocorrect: false,
            enableSuggestions: false,
            errorText: _scheduleError,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          Text(
            scheduleHint,
            style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(color: palette.inkFaint),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisField(
            label: 'Prompt',
            hint: 'What should Hermes do on each run?',
            controller: _prompt,
            minLines: 4,
            maxLines: 10,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
          IrisSettingsGroup(
            title: 'Run with',
            children: [
              IrisSettingsRow(
                label: 'Model',
                value: _model ?? 'Profile default',
                onTap: () => unawaited(_pickModel()),
              ),
              if (targets.isNotEmpty)
                IrisSettingsRow(
                  label: 'Deliver to',
                  sub: "Where each run's reply is sent",
                  value: deliverName ?? _deliver ?? 'Nowhere',
                  onTap: () => unawaited(_pickDeliver(targets)),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ModelChoice {
  const _ModelChoice(this.model, this.provider);
  final String? model;
  final String? provider;
}

class _DeliverChoice {
  const _DeliverChoice(this.id);
  final String? id;
}
```

```dart
      expect(durationText(const Duration(seconds: 20)), 'under a minute');
      expect(durationText(const Duration(minutes: 42)), '42m');
      expect(durationText(const Duration(hours: 9, minutes: 12)), '9h 12m');
      expect(durationText(const Duration(hours: 3)), '3h');
      expect(durationText(const Duration(days: 2, hours: 4)), '2d 4h');
    });

  group('statusOf', () {

        statusOf(fakeJob('a', state: CronState.running, lastError: 'x')),
        JobStatus.running,
      );
    });

    test('paused beats failed', () {
      expect(
        statusOf(fakeJob('a', enabled: false, lastError: 'x')),
        JobStatus.paused,
      );
    });

    test('a last error on an enabled job is failed', () {
      expect(statusOf(fakeJob('a', lastError: 'boom')), JobStatus.failed);
    });

    test('otherwise active', () {
      expect(statusOf(fakeJob('a')), JobStatus.active);
    });
  });

  group('statusLine', () {
    test('next run', () {
      expect(
        statusLine(
          fakeJob('a', nextRun: now.add(const Duration(hours: 9, minutes: 12))),
          now,
        ),
        'next in 9h 12m',
      );
    });

    test('paused', () {
      expect(statusLine(fakeJob('a', enabled: false), now), 'paused');
    });

    test('running', () {
      expect(
        statusLine(fakeJob('a', state: CronState.running), now),
        'running…',
      );
    });

    test('failed names when, and the next run if known', () {
      expect(
        statusLine(
          fakeJob(
            'a',
            lastError: 'boom',
            lastRun: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 2)),
            nextRun: now.add(const Duration(hours: 21, minutes: 40)),
          ),
          now,
        ),
        'failed 2h ago · next in 21h 40m',
      );
    });

    test('failed with no last run still says failed', () {
      expect(statusLine(fakeJob('a', lastError: 'boom'), now), 'failed');
    });

    test('a next run already past reads as due', () {
      expect(
        statusLine(
          fakeJob('a', nextRun: now.subtract(const Duration(minutes: 5))),
          now,
        ),
        'due now',
      );
    });

    test('no next run is an empty line, not a crash', () {
      expect(statusLine(fakeJob('a'), now), '');
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run and see them fail, then write `schedule_text.dart`**

Create `lib/features/scheduled/schedule_text.dart`:

```dart
/// The words a job card and a job's detail use for when it runs.
library;

import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../chat/relative_time.dart';

enum JobStatus { running, paused, failed, active }

JobStatus statusOf(CronJob job) {
  if (job.running) return JobStatus.running;
  if (!job.enabled || job.state == CronState.paused) return JobStatus.paused;
  if (job.lastError != null) return JobStatus.failed;
  return JobStatus.active;
}

/// "42m", "9h 12m", "2d 4h". Never longer than two units.
String durationText(Duration duration) {
  if (duration.inMinutes < 1) return 'under a minute';
  if (duration.inHours < 1) return '${duration.inMinutes}m';
  if (duration.inDays < 1) {
    final minutes = duration.inMinutes % 60;
    return minutes == 0
        ? '${duration.inHours}h'
        : '${duration.inHours}h ${minutes}m';
  }
  final hours = duration.inHours % 24;
  return hours == 0
      ? '${duration.inDays}d'
      : '${duration.inDays}d ${hours}h';
}

/// The status line under a job's schedule.
String statusLine(CronJob job, DateTime now) {
  final next = job.nextRun;
  final nextText = next == null
      ? null
      : (next.isAfter(now)
            ? 'next in ${durationText(next.difference(now))}'
            : 'due now');
  switch (statusOf(job)) {
    case JobStatus.running:
      return 'running…';
    case JobStatus.paused:
      return 'paused';
    case JobStatus.failed:
      final last = job.lastRun;
      final failed = last == null ? 'failed' : 'failed ${relativeTime(last, now)}';
      return nextText == null ? failed : '$failed · $nextText';
    case JobStatus.active:
      return nextText ?? '';
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';

    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final switches = find.byType(IrisSwitch);
    await tester.tap(switches.first);
    await tester.pump();
    expect(cron.calls, contains('setEnabled a false'));
    expect(
      tester.widget<IrisSwitch>(switches.first).value,
      isFalse,
      reason: 'shown before the server has answered',
    );
    cron.setEnabledGate!.complete();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  });

  testWidgets('a refused pause flips the switch back and says why', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    cron.writeResult = const Err(
      IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'job is running'),
    );
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.byType(IrisSwitch).first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.widget<IrisSwitch>(find.byType(IrisSwitch).first).value, isTrue);
    expect(find.text('job is running'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a server without the route says it is not supported', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    cron.writeResult = const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.byType(IrisSwitch).first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Not supported on this server'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  group('detail', () {
    CronJob failing() => fakeJob(
      'b',
      name: 'Nightly backup to cold storage',
      prompt: 'Back up /srv/data.',
      schedule: '0 2 * * *',
      lastError: 'backup target returned 502',
      lastRun: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 2)),
      model: 'opus-4.5',
      deliver: 'telegram-ops',
    );

    Widget detail() => tab(
      list: [failing()],
      child: JobDetailScreen(initial: failing()),
    );

    testWidgets('shows the error, prompt, model and delivery', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(detail());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Nightly backup to cold storage'), findsWidgets);
      expect(find.text('backup target returned 502'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Back up /srv/data.'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('opus-4.5'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('telegram-ops'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('a job with no error shows no error block', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(
        tab(
          list: [fakeJob('a', name: 'Fine', prompt: 'p')],
          child: JobDetailScreen(initial: fakeJob('a', name: 'Fine', prompt: 'p')),
        ),
      );
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Last error'), findsNothing);
      expect(find.text('Model'), findsNothing);
    });

    testWidgets('Run now triggers and says Started', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(detail());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Run now'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(cron.calls, contains('trigger b'));
      expect(find.text('Started'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('Delete asks first, then deletes', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(detail());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Delete'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.textContaining('Chats from past runs stay'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(cron.calls, isNot(contains('delete b')));
      await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Delete').last);
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(cron.calls, contains('delete b'));
    });

    testWidgets('past runs list the run chats', (tester) async {
      cron.runsResult = const Ok([
        ConversationSummary(id: 'run-1', title: 'Backup run'),
      ]);
      await tester.pumpWidget(detail());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Past runs'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Backup run'), findsOneWidget);
    });
  });
}
```

`find.text('Past runs')` matches because `IrisLabel` keeps the sentence-case string in its widget (it uppercases when painting). If the finder misses, use `find.bySemanticsLabel('Past runs')`.

Create `lib/features/scheduled/job_actions.dart`:

```dart
/// What a person can do to a job from a card or its detail.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';

/// Pauses or resumes [job]. The switch changes at once; if Hermes refuses, it
/// changes back and a toast says why.
Future<void> toggleJob(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref,
  CronJob job,
  bool enabled,
) async {
  final overrides = ref.read(cronEnabledOverridesProvider.notifier);
  overrides.set(job.id, enabled);
  final repository = await ref.read(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
  final result = await repository?.setEnabled(job.id, enabled: enabled);
  if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
    overrides.clear(job.id);
    if (context.mounted) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
    }
    return;
  }
  // Keep the optimistic value until the list has caught up, so the switch does
  // not flick back for a poll.
  ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider);
  try {
    await ref.read(cronJobsProvider.future);
  } on Object {
    // A failed refresh is the list's own business.
  }
  overrides.clear(job.id);
}

/// [job] with any pending pause or resume applied.
CronJob withOverride(CronJob job, Map<String, bool> overrides) =>
    overrides.containsKey(job.id)
    ? job.copyWith(enabled: overrides[job.id])
    : job;
```

Create `lib/features/scheduled/job_card.dart`:

```dart
/// One job in the Scheduled list (frame A2).
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/iris_orb.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';
import 'schedule_text.dart';

class JobCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const JobCard({
    required this.job,
    required this.now,
    required this.onOpen,
    required this.onToggle,
    super.key,
  });

  final CronJob job;
  final DateTime now;
  final VoidCallback onOpen;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onToggle;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final status = statusOf(job);
    final line = statusLine(job, now);
    final lineColor = status == JobStatus.failed
        ? palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed)
        : palette.inkMuted;
    return IrisPressable(
      onTap: onOpen,
      semanticLabel: '${job.title}, ${job.scheduleText}, $line',
      child: IrisCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
          IrisSpace.lg,
          IrisSpace.md,
          IrisSpace.sm,
          IrisSpace.md,
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    job.title,
                    maxLines: 2,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: type.body.copyWith(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      color: palette.ink,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: IrisSpace.sm,
                    runSpacing: IrisSpace.xs,
                    crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      // Verbatim and in mono: IrisChip would uppercase a cron
                      // expression.
                      DecoratedBox(
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: palette.surfaceSunken,
                          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(IrisRadius.xs),
                        ),
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: IrisSpace.sm - 1,
                            vertical: 3,
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            job.scheduleText,
                            style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(
                              fontSize: 11,
                              color: palette.inkMuted,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (line.isNotEmpty)
                        Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            if (status == JobStatus.running) ...[
                              const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.working, size: 16),
                              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.xs),
                            ],
                            Text(
                              line,
                              style: type.caption.copyWith(
                                fontSize: 11.5,
                                color: lineColor,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            // A 44 pt target around a 30 pt switch.
            GestureDetector(
              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
              onTap: () => onToggle(!job.enabled),
              child: SizedBox(
                width: 60,
                height: 44,
                child: Center(
                  child: IrisSwitch(
                    value: job.enabled,
                    semanticLabel: 'Enabled, ${job.title}',
                    onChanged: onToggle,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

If `IrisPressable` has no `semanticLabel` parameter in this codebase, check its constructor (`primitives.dart:201`); `IrisCircleButton` passes one, so it should. If it also lacks an `onTap` that works with a nested `GestureDetector`, the inner switch still wins the tap on its own area.

Create `lib/features/scheduled/scheduled_tab.dart`:

```dart
/// Home's Scheduled tab (frames A2, A3).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/iris_orb.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';
import 'job_actions.dart';
import 'job_card.dart';
import 'job_detail_screen.dart';
import 'job_form_screen.dart';

/// The floating Ask bar plus its fade: the list's last card scrolls clear of it.
const double _barClearance = 130;

class ScheduledTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ScheduledTab({super.key});

  Future<void> _newJob(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    final saved = await Navigator.of(context).push<CronJob>(
      IrisPageRoute<CronJob>(builder: (_) => const JobFormScreen()),
    );
    if (saved != null) ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final jobs = ref.watch(cronJobsProvider);
    final overrides = ref.watch(cronEnabledOverridesProvider);
    final profile = ref.watch(activeProfileProvider);
    final now = ref.read(clockProvider)();
    final list = jobs.value;

    if (list == null) {
      if (jobs.hasError) {
        final error = jobs.error;
        return IrisEmptyState(
          title: 'Could not load scheduled jobs',
          message: error is IrisFailure
              ? error.message ?? error.kind.name
              : '$error',
          action: IrisButton(
            label: 'Try again',
            onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider),
          ),
        );
      }
      return ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(gutter),
        children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) ...[
            const IrisSkeleton(width: double.infinity, height: 72),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          ],
        ],
      );
    }

    if (list.isEmpty) {
      return IrisEmptyState(
        title: 'No scheduled jobs on $profile',
        message: 'Jobs run a prompt on a schedule and keep each run as a chat.',
        icon: const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.idle, size: 72, glow: false),
        action: IrisButton(
          label: 'New job',
          tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
          onPressed: () => unawaited(_newJob(context, ref)),
        ),
      );
    }

    return RefreshIndicator(
      color: palette.accent,
      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceRaised,
      onRefresh: () async {
        ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider);
        try {
          await ref.read(cronJobsProvider.future);
        } on Object {
          // The list shows its own error.
        }
      },
      child: ListView(
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.md, gutter, _barClearance),
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              IrisLabel('${list.length} ${list.length == 1 ? 'job' : 'jobs'}'),
              if (jobs.isLoading) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.loading, size: 16),
                const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.xs),
                const IrisLabel('Refreshing', small: true),
              ],
              const Spacer(),
              IrisButton(
                label: 'New job',
                tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                onPressed: () => unawaited(_newJob(context, ref)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          for (final raw in list) ...[
            Builder(
              builder: (context) {
                final job = withOverride(raw, overrides);
                return JobCard(
                  job: job,
                  now: now,
                  onToggle: (enabled) =>
                      unawaited(toggleJob(context, ref, raw, enabled)),
                  onOpen: () => unawaited(
                    Navigator.of(context).push(
                      IrisPageRoute<void>(
                        builder: (_) => JobDetailScreen(initial: raw),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

If `IrisOrb` has no `glow` parameter, drop it. `IrisSkeleton(width: double.infinity, ...)` — use `width: 320` if infinity asserts.

Create `lib/features/scheduled/job_detail_screen.dart`:

```dart
/// One job: its state, its prompt, and what can be done to it (frame A4).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/iris_scaffold.dart';
import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';
import '../chat/chat_screen.dart';
import '../chat/delete_sheet.dart';
import '../chat/relative_time.dart';
import 'job_actions.dart';
import 'job_form_screen.dart';
import 'schedule_text.dart';

class JobDetailScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const JobDetailScreen({required this.initial, super.key});

  final CronJob initial;

  Future<void> _run(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref, CronJob job) async {
    final repository = await ref.read(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
    final result = await repository?.trigger(job.id);
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
      return;
    }
    showIrisToast(context, message: 'Started', top: true);
    ref
      ..invalidate(cronJobsProvider)
      ..invalidate(cronRunsProvider(job.id));
  }

  Future<void> _edit(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref, CronJob job) async {
    final saved = await Navigator.of(context).push<CronJob>(
      IrisPageRoute<CronJob>(builder: (_) => JobFormScreen(job: job)),
    );
    if (saved == null) return;
    ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider);
    if (context.mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
  }

  Future<void> _delete(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref, CronJob job) async {
    final confirmed = await showDeleteSheet(
      context,
      job.title,
      message:
          'This removes the job from Hermes for good. Chats from past runs stay.',
    );
    if (!confirmed || !context.mounted) return;
    final repository = await ref.read(cronRepositoryProvider.future);
    final result = await repository?.delete(job.id);
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
      return;
    }
    ref.invalidate(cronJobsProvider);
    Navigator.of(context).pop();
    showIrisToast(context, message: 'Deleted ‘${job.title}’');
  }

  Future<void> _openRun(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    ConversationSummary run,
  ) async {
    final chat = await ref.read(activeChatProvider.future);
    if (chat == null || !context.mounted) return;
    await Navigator.of(context).push(
      IrisPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (_) => ChatScreen(
          backend: chat,
          conversationId: run.id,
          title: run.title,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final overrides = ref.watch(cronEnabledOverridesProvider);
    final fromList = ref
        .watch(cronJobsProvider)
        .value
        ?.where((j) => j.id == initial.id)
        .firstOrNull;
    final job = withOverride(fromList ?? initial, overrides);
    final now = ref.read(clockProvider)();
    final runs = ref.watch(cronRunsProvider(job.id));
    final failed = palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed);
    final line = statusLine(job, now);

    return IrisScaffold(
      header: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.sm, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            IrisCircleButton(
              icon: Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              semanticLabel: 'Back',
              onPressed: () => unawaited(Navigator.of(context).maybePop()),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                job.title,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: type.heading.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.md, gutter, IrisSpace.xxl),
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              DecoratedBox(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: palette.surfaceSunken,
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(IrisRadius.xs),
                ),
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: IrisSpace.sm,
                    vertical: 3,
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    job.scheduleText,
                    style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  line,
                  style: type.caption.copyWith(
                    color: statusOf(job) == JobStatus.failed
                        ? failed
                        : palette.inkMuted,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisSettingsGroup(
            title: 'Status',
            children: [
              IrisSettingsRow(
                label: 'Enabled',
                trailing: IrisSwitch(
                  value: job.enabled,
                  semanticLabel: 'Enabled',
                  onChanged: (enabled) =>
                      unawaited(toggleJob(context, ref, fromList ?? initial, enabled)),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (job.lastError case final error?) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            IrisCard(
              accent: failed,
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  IrisLabel('Last error', color: failed),
                  const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
                  Text(error, style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.ink)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
          if (job.prompt case final prompt?) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            IrisLabel('Prompt', color: palette.inkFaint),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
            Text(prompt, style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.ink)),
          ],
          if (job.model != null || job.deliver != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            IrisSettingsGroup(
              title: 'Run with',
              children: [
                if (job.model case final model?)
                  IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Model', value: model),
                if (job.deliver case final deliver?)
                  IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Delivers to', value: deliver),
              ],
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
          IrisButton(
            label: 'Run now',
            tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
            expand: true,
            onPressed: () => unawaited(_run(context, ref, job)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          IrisButton(
            label: 'Edit',
            expand: true,
            onPressed: () => unawaited(_edit(context, ref, job)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          IrisButton(
            label: 'Delete',
            tone: IrisButtonTone.danger,
            expand: true,
            onPressed: () => unawaited(_delete(context, ref, job)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
          IrisLabel('Past runs', color: palette.inkFaint),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          switch (runs) {
            AsyncData(:final value) when value.isEmpty => Text(
              'No runs yet. Run now starts one.',
              style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
            AsyncData(:final value) => IrisSettingsGroup(
              title: 'Newest first',
              children: [
                for (final run in value)
                  IrisSettingsRow(
                    label: run.title,
                    sub: run.startedAt == null
                        ? null
                        : relativeTime(run.startedAt!, now),
                    onTap: () => unawaited(_openRun(context, ref, run)),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
            AsyncError() => Text(
              "Couldn't load past runs.",
              style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
            _ => const IrisSkeleton(width: 220, height: 16),
          },
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

The detail screen is pushed from inside a `ProviderScope`, so `ref.watch(cronJobsProvider)` keeps the polled list alive while it is open, which keeps the Enabled switch current.

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/tasks/move_sheet.dart';

import '../support/fake_automation.dart';
import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  const columns = ['triage', 'todo', 'ready', 'running', 'blocked', 'review', 'done'];
  final task = fakeTask('t_31', title: 'Backup failing', status: 'blocked');

  Future<void> open(WidgetTester tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      themed(
        Builder(
          builder: (context) => Center(
            child: TextButton(
              onPressed: () => showMoveSheet(
                context,
                task: task,
                columns: columns,
              ),
              child: const Text('open'),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  }

  testWidgets('names the task and lists the columns', (tester) async {
    await open(tester);
    expect(find.text('Move t_31'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Backup failing'), findsOneWidget);
    for (final label in ['Triage', 'To do', 'Ready', 'Blocked', 'Done']) {
      expect(find.text(label), findsOneWidget, reason: label);
    }
  });

  testWidgets('Running and Review say they are set by Hermes', (tester) async {
    await open(tester);
    expect(find.text('Set by Hermes'), findsNWidgets(2));
    expect(find.byIcon(Icons.lock_outline), findsNWidgets(2));
  });

  testWidgets('a locked row does not respond to a tap', (tester) async {
    await open(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Running'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Move t_31'), findsOneWidget, reason: 'sheet still open');
  });

  testWidgets('tapping a free column returns its id', (tester) async {
    String? result;
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      themed(
        Builder(
          builder: (context) => Center(
            child: TextButton(
              onPressed: () async => result = await showMoveSheet(
                context,
                task: task,
                columns: columns,
              ),
              child: const Text('open'),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('To do'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(result, 'todo');
  });

  testWidgets('tapping the current column closes without a move', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    String? result = 'untouched';
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      themed(
        Builder(
          builder: (context) => Center(
            child: TextButton(
              onPressed: () async => result = await showMoveSheet(
                context,
                task: task,
                columns: columns,
              ),
              child: const Text('open'),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Blocked'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(result, isNull);
  });

  testWidgets('an unknown column shows under its own name', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      themed(
        Builder(
          builder: (context) => Center(
            child: TextButton(
              onPressed: () => showMoveSheet(
                context,
                task: task,
                columns: [...columns, 'sprinting'],
              ),
              child: const Text('open'),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Sprinting'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

Create `lib/features/tasks/move_sheet.dart`:

```dart
/// Choosing a column to move a task to (frame B4).
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

/// The chosen column id, or null when the sheet was dismissed or the current
/// column was tapped. Columns Hermes sets itself are shown but do not respond.
Future<String?> showMoveSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required KanbanTask task,
  required List<String> columns,
}) => showIrisSheet<String>(
  context: context,
  builder: (sheetContext) {
    final palette = sheetContext.palette;
    return IrisSheet(
      title: 'Move ${task.id}',
      subtitle: task.title,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          for (final id in columns)
            Builder(
              builder: (context) {
                final locked = KanbanColumns.isSystemSet(id);
                final current = id == task.status;
                return Opacity(
                  opacity: locked ? 0.55 : 1,
                  child: IrisListRow(
                    title: KanbanColumns.label(id),
                    subtitle: locked ? 'Set by Hermes' : null,
                    selected: current,
                    leading: locked
                        ? Icon(
                            Icons.lock_outline,
                            size: IrisSize.iconSmall,
                            color: palette.inkMuted,
                          )
                        : null,
                    onTap: locked
                        ? null
                        : () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(
                            current ? null : id,
                          ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  },
);
```

Run the test again. Expected: PASS. If `IrisListRow` with a null `onTap` still reports a press response, that is acceptable as long as the tap does not pop; the "does not respond" test only checks the sheet stays open.

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/automation_providers.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/providers.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/failure.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/design/components/controls.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/tasks/task_form_screen.dart';

import '../support/fake_automation.dart';
import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeKanbanRepository kanban;
  setUp(() => kanban = FakeKanbanRepository());

  Widget form({String initialColumn = 'triage'}) => ProviderScope(
    overrides: [
      kanbanRepositoryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => kanban),
      profilesProvider.overrideWith(
        (ref) async => const [
          AgentProfile(name: 'default', isDefault: true),
          AgentProfile(name: 'homelab'),
        ],
      ),
    ],
    child: themed(TaskFormScreen(initialColumn: initialColumn)),
  );

  Finder field(String label) => find.byWidgetPredicate(
    (w) => w is IrisField && w.label == label,
  );

  bool createEnabled(WidgetTester tester) =>
      tester
          .widget<IrisButton>(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Create'))
          .onPressed !=
      null;

  testWidgets('Create waits for a title', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(createEnabled(tester), isFalse);
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), '  ');
    await tester.pump();
    expect(createEnabled(tester), isFalse);
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), 'Rotate the signing secret');
    await tester.pump();
    expect(createEnabled(tester), isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('starts in the column it was opened from', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form(initialColumn: 'todo'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), 'x');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Create'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(kanban.drafts.single.status, 'todo');
  });

  testWidgets('a column the person cannot create in falls back to Triage', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form(initialColumn: 'review'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), 'x');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Create'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(kanban.drafts.single.status, 'triage');
  });

  testWidgets('sends the column, assignee, priority and body', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), 'Rotate the secret');
    await tester.enterText(field('Body · optional · Markdown'), 'and verify');
    await tester.tap(find.text('To do'));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('More priority'));
    await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('More priority'));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Assignee'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('homelab'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Create'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final draft = kanban.drafts.single;
    expect(draft.title, 'Rotate the secret');
    expect(draft.body, 'and verify');
    expect(draft.status, 'todo');
    expect(draft.priority, 2);
    expect(draft.assignee, 'homelab');
  });

  testWidgets('priority is optional, and less than 1 clears it', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Optional'), findsWidgets);
    await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('More priority'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('1'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Less priority'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('1'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('a refused create keeps the form and says why', (tester) async {
    kanban.createResult = const Err(
      IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'title too long'),
    );
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.enterText(field('Title'), 'x');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Create'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('title too long'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(TaskFormScreen), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('priority buttons are at least 44 pt', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(form());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    for (final tip in ['More priority', 'Less priority']) {
      final size = tester.getSize(find.byTooltip(tip));
      expect(size.width, greaterThanOrEqualTo(44), reason: tip);
      expect(size.height, greaterThanOrEqualTo(44), reason: tip);
    }
  });
}
```

```dart
/// A new task (frame B5).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/segmented.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/iris_scaffold.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';

class TaskFormScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const TaskFormScreen({this.initialColumn = 'triage', super.key});

  /// The column the board was showing. Only Triage and To do can be created
  /// in; anything else starts in Triage.
  final String initialColumn;

  @override
  ConsumerState<TaskFormScreen> createState() => _TaskFormScreenState();
}

class _TaskFormScreenState extends ConsumerState<TaskFormScreen> {
  final _title = TextEditingController();
  final _body = TextEditingController();
  late String _column = KanbanColumns.creatable.contains(widget.initialColumn)
      ? widget.initialColumn
      : 'triage';
  String? _assignee;
  int? _priority;
  bool _saving = false;

  bool get _canCreate => !_saving && _title.text.trim().isNotEmpty;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _title.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _title.dispose();
    _body.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _step(int by) => setState(() {
    final next = (_priority ?? 0) + by;
    _priority = next < 1 ? null : next;
  });

  Future<void> _pickAssignee() async {
    final profiles = ref.read(profilesProvider).value ?? const [];
    final choice = await showIrisSheet<_Assignee>(
      context: context,
      builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
        title: 'Assignee',
        subtitle: 'The profile that works on this task.',
        child: Column(
          children: [
            IrisListRow(
              title: 'None',
              selected: _assignee == null,
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(const _Assignee(null)),
            ),
            for (final profile in profiles)
              IrisListRow(
                title: profile.name,
                selected: _assignee == profile.name,
                onTap: () =>
                    Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(_Assignee(profile.name)),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (choice != null && mounted) setState(() => _assignee = choice.name);
  }

  Future<void> _create() async {
    final repository = await ref.read(kanbanRepositoryProvider.future);
    if (repository == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    final result = await repository.create(
      TaskDraft(
        title: _title.text,
        body: _body.text,
        status: _column,
        assignee: _assignee,
        priority: _priority,
      ),
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        ref.invalidate(kanbanBoardProvider);
        Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
      case Err(:final failure):
        setState(() => _saving = false);
        showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    return IrisScaffold(
      header: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.sm, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Cancel',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                'New task',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: type.heading.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
              ),
            ),
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Create',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
              busy: _saving,
              onPressed: _canCreate ? () => unawaited(_create()) : null,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.lg, gutter, IrisSpace.xxl),
        children: [
          IrisField(
            label: 'Title',
            hint: 'What needs doing',
            controller: _title,
            autofocus: true,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisField(
            label: 'Body · optional · Markdown',
            hint: "What needs doing, and how you'll know it's done",
            controller: _body,
            minLines: 4,
            maxLines: 10,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisLabel('Column', color: palette.inkFaint),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          IrisSegmented<String>(
            values: KanbanColumns.creatable,
            selected: _column,
            labelOf: KanbanColumns.label,
            onChanged: (column) => setState(() => _column = column),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
          IrisSettingsGroup(
            title: 'Details',
            children: [
              IrisSettingsRow(
                label: 'Assignee',
                sub: 'Optional · a profile',
                value: _assignee ?? 'None',
                onTap: () => unawaited(_pickAssignee()),
              ),
              IrisSettingsRow(
                label: 'Priority',
                sub: _priority == null ? 'Optional' : null,
                trailing: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    _StepButton(
                      icon: Icons.remove,
                      tooltip: 'Less priority',
                      onPressed: _priority == null ? null : () => _step(-1),
                    ),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 28,
                      child: Text(
                        _priority?.toString() ?? '',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: type.code.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
                      ),
                    ),
                    _StepButton(
                      icon: Icons.add,
                      tooltip: 'More priority',
                      onPressed: () => _step(1),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Assignee {
  const _Assignee(this.name);
  final String? name;
}

class _StepButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _StepButton({
    required this.icon,
    required this.tooltip,
    required this.onPressed,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String tooltip;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Tooltip(
    message: tooltip,
    child: GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: onPressed,
      child: SizedBox(
        width: 44,
        height: 44,
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          size: IrisSize.icon,
          color: onPressed == null
              ? context.palette.inkFaint
              : context.palette.ink,
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the detail provider**

Append to `lib/app/automation_providers.dart`:

```dart
/// One task with its comments, runs and links.
final kanbanTaskDetailProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<KanbanTaskDetail, String>((ref, id) async {
      final repository = await ref.watch(kanbanRepositoryProvider.future);
      if (repository == null) {
        throw const IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound);
      }
      return switch (await repository.task(id)) {
        Ok(:final value) => value,
        Err(:final failure) => throw failure,
      };
    });
```

and add `import '../core/failure.dart';` at the top.

```dart
            't_30',
            title: 'Investigate heartbeat gaps',
            status: 'running',
            assignee: 'homelab',
            priority: 2,
            summary: 'Pulled the logs',
            startedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(minutes: 4)),
            progress: const KanbanProgress(2, 5),
            comments: 3,
            warnings: 1,
          ),
          fakeTask('t_28', title: 'Refactor billing', status: 'running'),
        ],
      ),
      KanbanColumn(
        id: 'blocked',

          fakeTask(
            't_31',
            title: 'Backup failing',
            status: 'blocked',
            blockKind: 'needs_input',
          ),

            't_32',

            status: 'blocked',

          ),

      ),
      const KanbanColumn(id: 'review', tasks: []),

  Widget tab({KanbanBoard? shown, Widget child = const TasksTab()}) =>

  ) async {

    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

  });

    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Flaky CI'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Investigate heartbeat gaps'), findsNothing);

  testWidgets('a running card shows the elapsed clock and subtasks, no percent', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Running').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Investigate heartbeat gaps'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('4m'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('2 of 5 subtasks'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Pulled the logs'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('p2'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('homelab'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('%'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.textContaining('due'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('a running card with no optional fields shows only its title', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Running').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Refactor billing'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('t_28'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('blocked cards show their kind verbatim', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Blocked').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('needs input'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('dependency'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a system-set empty column says Hermes moves tasks there', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Review').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Nothing in Review'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text('Hermes moves tasks here. You can’t add one yourself.'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('a person-movable empty column offers New task', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('To do').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Nothing in To do'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'New task'), findsWidgets);
  });

  testWidgets('an empty board says there are no tasks', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab(shown: const KanbanBoard(columns: [])));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('No tasks yet'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('no overflow at 320 pt wide', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(320, 700);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
    await tester.pumpWidget(tab());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Running').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
  });

  group('detail', () {
    KanbanTaskDetail detail({String? sessionId}) => KanbanTaskDetail(
      task: fakeTask(
        't_26',
        title: 'Rotate gateway API keys',
        status: 'review',
        assignee: 'homelab',
        priority: 2,
        sessionId: sessionId,
      ),
      result: 'New keys are live.',
      lastFailure: 'webhook-relay: health check timed out',
      comments: const [KanbanComment(author: 'you', body: 'Hold off')],
      runs: const [KanbanRun(outcome: 'completed')],
      parents: const [KanbanLink(id: 't_20', title: 'Q4 hygiene')],
      children: const [
        KanbanLink(id: 't_34', title: 'Update api-edge', status: 'done'),
      ],
    );

    Widget screen({String? sessionId}) {
      kanban.detailResult = Ok(detail(sessionId: sessionId));
      return tab(
        child: TaskDetailScreen(
          taskId: 't_26',
          initial: fakeTask('t_26', title: 'Rotate gateway API keys'),
        ),
      );
    }

    testWidgets('shows the result, failure, links, comments and runs', (
      tester,
    ) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(screen());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Rotate gateway API keys'), findsWidgets);
      expect(find.text('New keys are live.'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('webhook-relay: health check timed out'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.textContaining('Q4 hygiene'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Update api-edge'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Hold off'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('completed'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('Open session shows only when the task links to one', (
      tester,
    ) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(screen());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Open session'), findsNothing);
      await tester.pumpWidget(screen(sessionId: 'sess-1'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Open session'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('Move sends the new column', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(screen());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Move'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.text('Done'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(kanban.calls, contains('move t_26 done'));
    });

    testWidgets('a refused move says why and changes nothing', (tester) async {
      kanban.moveResult = const Err(
        IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'blocked by a parent'),
      );
      await tester.pumpWidget(screen());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(IrisButton, 'Move'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.text('Done'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('blocked by a parent'), findsOneWidget);
    });

    testWidgets('a detail that fails to load says so and keeps the title', (
      tester,
    ) async {
      kanban.detailResult = const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.server));
      await tester.pumpWidget(
        tab(
          child: TaskDetailScreen(
            taskId: 't_26',
            initial: fakeTask('t_26', title: 'Rotate gateway API keys'),
          ),
        ),
      );
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Rotate gateway API keys'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Could not load this task'), findsOneWidget);
    });
  });
}
```

```dart
/// One task on the board (frames B1, B1·B).
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/iris_orb.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';

/// "4m", "1h 12m": a run clock. Under a minute reads "<1m".
String elapsedText(DateTime startedAt, DateTime now) {
  final elapsed = now.difference(startedAt);
  if (elapsed.inMinutes < 1) return '<1m';
  if (elapsed.inHours < 1) return '${elapsed.inMinutes}m';
  final minutes = elapsed.inMinutes % 60;
  return minutes == 0
      ? '${elapsed.inHours}h'
      : '${elapsed.inHours}h ${minutes}m';
}

/// `needs_input` as "needs input". The wording is the server's, not Iris's.
String blockKindText(String kind) => kind.replaceAll('_', ' ');

class TaskCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const TaskCard({
    required this.task,
    required this.now,
    required this.onOpen,
    super.key,
  });

  final KanbanTask task;
  final DateTime now;
  final VoidCallback onOpen;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final started = task.startedAt;
    final blockKind = task.status == 'blocked' ? task.blockKind : null;
    final meta = type.codeSmall.copyWith(
      fontSize: 11,
      color: palette.inkFaint,
    );
    return IrisPressable(
      onTap: onOpen,
      semanticLabel: '${task.title}, ${KanbanColumns.label(task.status)}',
      child: IrisCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: IrisSpace.lg,
          vertical: IrisSpace.md,
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                if (task.running) ...[
                  const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.working, size: 22),
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                ],
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    task.title,
                    maxLines: 3,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: type.body.copyWith(
                      fontSize: 14.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      color: palette.ink,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (task.running && started != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                  Text(
                    elapsedText(started, now),
                    style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(
                      color: palette.colorFor(AgentState.working),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
            if (blockKind != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: blockKind == 'needs_input'
                    ? IrisChip.state(
                        blockKindText(blockKind),
                        AgentState.waiting,
                        palette,
                      )
                    : IrisChip(blockKindText(blockKind)),
              ),
            ],
            if (task.latestSummary case final summary?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              Text(
                summary,
                maxLines: 2,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: type.caption.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
              ),
            ],
            if (task.progress case final progress?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
              Text(
                '${progress.done} of ${progress.total} subtasks',
                style: type.caption.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
            Wrap(
              spacing: IrisSpace.md,
              runSpacing: IrisSpace.xs,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Text(task.id, style: meta),
                if (task.assignee case final who?) Text(who, style: meta),
                if (task.priority case final priority?)
                  Text('p$priority', style: meta),
                if (task.commentCount > 0)
                  Text(
                    '${task.commentCount} ${task.commentCount == 1 ? 'comment' : 'comments'}',
                    style: meta,
                  ),
                if (task.warningCount > 0)
                  Text(
                    '${task.warningCount} ${task.warningCount == 1 ? 'warning' : 'warnings'}',
                    style: meta.copyWith(
                      color: palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/tasks/task_actions.dart`:

```dart
/// Moving a task: shown at once, rolled back if Hermes refuses.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';

Future<void> moveTask(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref,
  KanbanTask task,
  String status,
) async {
  // Hermes owns these columns, and moving to where a task already is does
  // nothing.
  if (KanbanColumns.isSystemSet(status) || status == task.status) return;
  final overrides = ref.read(taskMoveOverridesProvider.notifier);
  overrides.set(task.id, status);
  final repository = await ref.read(kanbanRepositoryProvider.future);
  final result = await repository?.move(task.id, status);
  if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
    overrides.clear(task.id);
    if (context.mounted) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
    }
    return;
  }
  // Keep the move on screen until the board has caught up with it.
  ref
    ..invalidate(kanbanBoardProvider)
    ..invalidate(kanbanTaskDetailProvider(task.id));
  try {
    await ref.read(kanbanBoardProvider.future);
  } on Object {
    // A failed refresh is the board's own business.
  }
  overrides.clear(task.id);
}

/// [board] with every pending move applied.
KanbanBoard withMoves(KanbanBoard board, Map<String, String> moves) {
  var shown = board;
  for (final entry in moves.entries) {
    shown = shown.moved(entry.key, entry.value);
  }
  return shown;
}
```
