```dart
/// Home's Tasks tab: column chips, then the selected column's cards
/// (frames B1, B2).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/iris_orb.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';
import 'task_actions.dart';
import 'task_card.dart';
import 'task_detail_screen.dart';
import 'task_form_screen.dart';

const double _barClearance = 130;

class TasksTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const TasksTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<TasksTab> createState() => _TasksTabState();
}

class _TasksTabState extends ConsumerState<TasksTab> {
  /// The column the person picked; null follows the first that has cards.
  String? _picked;

  Future<void> _newTask(String column) async {
    await Navigator.of(context).push<bool>(
      IrisPageRoute<bool>(
        builder: (_) => TaskFormScreen(initialColumn: column),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final async = ref.watch(kanbanBoardProvider);
    final moves = ref.watch(taskMoveOverridesProvider);
    final now = ref.read(clockProvider)();
    final raw = async.value;

    if (raw == null) {
      if (async.hasError) {
        final error = async.error;
        return IrisEmptyState(
          title: 'Could not load tasks',
          message: error is IrisFailure
              ? error.message ?? error.kind.name
              : '$error',
          action: IrisButton(
            label: 'Try again',
            onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(kanbanBoardProvider),
          ),
        );
      }
      return ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(gutter),
        children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) ...[
            const IrisSkeleton(width: double.infinity, height: 84),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          ],
        ],
      );
    }

    final board = withMoves(raw, moves);
    if (board.columns.isEmpty) {
      return IrisEmptyState(
        title: 'No tasks yet',
        message: 'Tasks you add, and ones Hermes creates, show up here.',
        icon: const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.idle, size: 72, glow: false),
        action: IrisButton(
          label: 'New task',
          tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
          onPressed: () => unawaited(_newTask('triage')),
        ),
      );
    }

    final selected = board.column(_picked ?? '') ??
        board.columns.firstWhere(
          (column) => column.tasks.isNotEmpty,
          orElse: () => board.columns.first,
        );
    final systemSet = KanbanColumns.isSystemSet(selected.id);

    return RefreshIndicator(
      color: palette.accent,
      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceRaised,
      onRefresh: () async {
        ref.invalidate(kanbanBoardProvider);
        try {
          await ref.read(kanbanBoardProvider.future);
        } on Object {
          // The board shows its own error.
        }
      },
      child: ListView(
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0, IrisSpace.md, 0, _barClearance),
        children: [
          SingleChildScrollView(
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: gutter),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                for (final column in board.columns) ...[
                  _ColumnChip(
                    label: column.label,
                    count: column.tasks.length,
                    selected: column.id == selected.id,
                    onTap: () {
                      unawaited(IrisHaptics.selection());
                      setState(() => _picked = column.id);
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.lg, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                IrisLabel(
                  systemSet
                      ? '${selected.label} · set by Hermes'
                      : '${selected.label} · ${selected.tasks.length}',
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                IrisButton(
                  label: 'New task',
                  tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                  onPressed: () => unawaited(_newTask(selected.id)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (selected.tasks.isEmpty)
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: gutter),
              child: IrisEmptyState(
                title: 'Nothing in ${selected.label}',
                message: systemSet
                    ? 'Hermes moves tasks here. You can’t add one yourself.'
                    : 'Tasks you move or add here show up in this column.',
                icon: const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.idle, size: 72, glow: false),
                action: systemSet
                    ? null
                    : IrisButton(
                        label: 'New task',
                        tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
                        onPressed: () => unawaited(_newTask(selected.id)),
                      ),
              ),
            )
          else
            for (final task in selected.tasks)
              Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, 0, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
                child: TaskCard(
                  task: task,
                  now: now,
                  onOpen: () => unawaited(
                    Navigator.of(context).push(
                      IrisPageRoute<void>(
                        builder: (_) =>
                            TaskDetailScreen(taskId: task.id, initial: task),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ColumnChip extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ColumnChip({
    required this.label,
    required this.count,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String label;
  final int count;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    return IrisPressable(
      onTap: onTap,
      semanticLabel: '$label, $count${selected ? ', selected' : ''}',
      child: Container(
        height: 32,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: IrisSpace.md),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? palette.ink : palette.surface,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(IrisRadius.full),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selected ? palette.ink : palette.line,
            width: IrisStroke.hairline,
          ),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(
              label,
              style: type.body.copyWith(
                fontSize: 12.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                color: selected ? palette.inkInverse : palette.inkMuted,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.xs + 2),
            Text(
              '$count',
              style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(
                fontSize: 11,
                color: (selected ? palette.inkInverse : palette.inkMuted)
                    .withValues(alpha: 0.7),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
/// One task: what it is, how it went, and where it can go (frame B3).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/markdown.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/iris_scaffold.dart';
import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';
import '../chat/chat_screen.dart';
import '../chat/relative_time.dart';
import 'move_sheet.dart';
import 'task_actions.dart';
import 'task_card.dart';

class TaskDetailScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const TaskDetailScreen({required this.taskId, this.initial, super.key});

  final String taskId;

  /// The card the person tapped, drawn while the detail loads.
  final KanbanTask? initial;

  Future<void> _move(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    KanbanTask task,
  ) async {
    final columns =
        ref.read(kanbanBoardProvider).value?.columns.map((c) => c.id).toList() ??
        const ['triage', 'todo', 'ready', 'running', 'blocked', 'review', 'done'];
    final picked = await showMoveSheet(context, task: task, columns: columns);
    if (picked == null || !context.mounted) return;
    await moveTask(context, ref, task, picked);
  }

  Future<void> _openSession(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    KanbanTask task,
  ) async {
    final chat = await ref.read(activeChatProvider.future);
    if (chat == null || !context.mounted) return;
    await Navigator.of(context).push(
      IrisPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (_) => ChatScreen(
          backend: chat,
          conversationId: task.sessionId,
          title: task.title,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final detail = ref.watch(kanbanTaskDetailProvider(taskId));
    final moves = ref.watch(taskMoveOverridesProvider);
    final now = ref.read(clockProvider)();
    final loaded = detail.value;
    var task = loaded?.task ?? initial;
    if (task != null && moves.containsKey(task.id)) {
      task = task.copyWith(status: moves[task.id]);
    }
    final failed = palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed);

    return IrisScaffold(
      header: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.sm, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            IrisCircleButton(
              icon: Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              semanticLabel: 'Back',
              onPressed: () => unawaited(Navigator.of(context).maybePop()),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
            Text(taskId, style: type.code.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted)),
            const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
            if (task != null)
              IrisChip(KanbanColumns.label(task.status)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.md, gutter, IrisSpace.xxl),
        children: [
          if (task == null)
            const IrisSkeleton(width: 220, height: 20)
          else ...[
            Text(
              task.title,
              style: type.title.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
            Wrap(
              spacing: IrisSpace.md,
              children: [
                if (task.assignee case final who?)
                  Text(who, style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted)),
                if (task.priority case final priority?)
                  Text(
                    'priority $priority',
                    style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
          if (detail.hasError && loaded == null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            Text(
              'Could not load this task',
              style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
          ],
          if (loaded != null) ...[
            for (final parent in loaded.parents) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
              IrisPressable(
                onTap: () => unawaited(
                  Navigator.of(context).push(
                    IrisPageRoute<void>(
                      builder: (_) => TaskDetailScreen(taskId: parent.id),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  'Parent · ${parent.id} ${parent.title}',
                  style: type.caption.copyWith(color: palette.accent),
                ),
              ),
            ],
            if (loaded.task.body case final body?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              IrisMarkdown(body),
            ],
            if (loaded.task.latestSummary case final summary?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              IrisLabel('Latest summary', color: palette.inkFaint),
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
              Text(summary, style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.ink)),
            ],
            if (loaded.result case final result?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              IrisLabel('Result', color: palette.inkFaint),
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
              IrisMarkdown(result),
            ],
            if (loaded.lastFailure case final failure?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              IrisCard(
                accent: failed,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    IrisLabel('Last failure', color: failed),
                    const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
                    Text(failure, style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.ink)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
            if (loaded.children.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
              IrisSettingsGroup(
                title: 'Subtasks · ${loaded.children.length}',
                children: [
                  for (final child in loaded.children)
                    IrisSettingsRow(
                      label: child.title,
                      sub: child.id,
                      value: child.status == null
                          ? null
                          : KanbanColumns.label(child.status!),
                      onTap: () => unawaited(
                        Navigator.of(context).push(
                          IrisPageRoute<void>(
                            builder: (_) => TaskDetailScreen(taskId: child.id),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
            if (loaded.comments.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
              IrisLabel('Comments · ${loaded.comments.length}', color: palette.inkFaint),
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              for (final comment in loaded.comments) ...[
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      comment.author,
                      style: type.caption.copyWith(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: palette.ink,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (comment.at case final at?) ...[
                      const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                      Text(
                        relativeTime(at, now),
                        style: type.caption.copyWith(color: palette.inkFaint),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(comment.body, style: type.body.copyWith(color: palette.ink)),
                const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
              ],
            ],
            if (loaded.runs.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              IrisSettingsGroup(
                title: 'Runs · ${loaded.runs.length}',
                children: [
                  for (final run in loaded.runs)
                    IrisSettingsRow(
                      label: run.startedAt == null
                          ? 'Run'
                          : _clock(run.startedAt!, run.endedAt),
                      value: run.outcome,
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ],
          if (task != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Move',
              expand: true,
              onPressed: () => unawaited(_move(context, ref, task!)),
            ),
            if (task.sessionId != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              IrisButton(
                label: 'Open session',
                tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
                expand: true,
                onPressed: () => unawaited(_openSession(context, ref, task!)),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _clock(DateTime start, DateTime? end) {
    String hm(DateTime t) =>
        '${t.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    return end == null ? hm(start) : '${hm(start)} → ${hm(end)}';
  }
}
```

`elapsedText` import is unused in this file; drop `task_card.dart` import if the analyzer flags it. The `Parent` link line reads "Parent · t_20 Q4 hygiene", which the test finds with `textContaining`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Add `compress` and `rename` to the controller**

In `lib/features/chat/chat_controller.dart`, directly after `undo()`:

```dart
  /// Summarises the conversation to free context. Needs a live, idle chat.
  Future<void> compress() async {
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    void say(String message) => onToast?.call(message);
    if (runtimeId == null) {
      say('Nothing to compress yet');
      return;
    }
    if (value.running) {
      say('Stop the reply first to compress.');
      return;
    }
    switch (await _backend.compress(runtimeId)) {
      case Err(:final failure):
        if (_disposed) return;
        say(
          (failure.rpcError?.isMethodNotFound ?? false)
              ? "Compress isn't supported on this server."
              : 'Could not compress: ${describe(failure)}',
        );
      case Ok():
        if (!_disposed) say('Compressed');
    }
  }

  /// Renames the conversation. The title changes at once and changes back,
  /// with a toast, if Hermes refuses.
  Future<void> rename(String title) async {
    final id = _storedId;
    if (id == null) return;
    final previous = value.title;
    _update(value.copyWith(title: title));
    final result = await _backend.rename(id, title);
    if (_disposed) return;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      if (previous != null) _update(value.copyWith(title: previous));
      onToast?.call('Could not rename: ${describe(failure)}');
    }
  }
```

Run: `flutter test test/features/chat_controller_test.dart` → PASS.

```dart
    await open(tester);
    final delete = tester.getTopLeft(find.text('Delete')).dy;
    for (final label in ['Rename', 'Branch', 'Session info', 'Export as Markdown']) {
      expect(tester.getTopLeft(find.text(label)).dy, lessThan(delete));
    }
  });

  testWidgets('fits at 320 pt wide with large text', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(320, 700);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
    await open(tester, replying: true);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the menu**

Create `lib/features/chat/chat_menu_sheet.dart`:

```dart
/// The chat header's ⋯ menu (frames C1, C1·B).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';

enum ChatMenuAction {
  rename,
  pin,
  branch,
  archive,
  compress,
  undo,
  export,
  info,
  delete,
}

/// The chosen action, or null when dismissed. Rows that rewrite history are
/// dimmed with a reason while the agent replies, not hidden.
Future<ChatMenuAction?> showChatMenu(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String title,
  required bool pinned,
  required bool replying,
  required bool live,
  required bool canPin,
  required bool canArchive,
  required bool canDelete,
  required bool canUndo,
}) {
  unawaited(IrisHaptics.selection());
  return showIrisSheet<ChatMenuAction>(
    context: context,
    builder: (sheetContext) {
      final palette = sheetContext.palette;

      Widget row(
        ChatMenuAction action,
        String label,
        IconData icon, {
        String? disabledReason,
        bool danger = false,
      }) {
        final enabled = disabledReason == null;
        final tint = danger
            ? palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed)
            : palette.inkMuted;
        return Opacity(
          opacity: enabled ? 1 : 0.55,
          child: IrisListRow(
            title: label,
            subtitle: disabledReason,
            leading: Icon(
              icon,
              size: IrisSize.icon,
              color: enabled ? tint : palette.inkFaint,
            ),
            onTap: enabled ? () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(action) : null,
          ),
        );
      }

      String? needsIdle() => replying ? 'Stop the reply first' : null;
      String? needsLive() => replying
          ? 'Stop the reply first'
          : (live ? null : 'Not connected yet');

      Widget group(String name, List<Widget> rows) => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.md, bottom: IrisSpace.xs),
            child: IrisLabel(name, color: palette.inkFaint),
          ),
          ...rows,
        ],
      );

      return IrisSheet(
        title: title,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            group('Conversation', [
              row(ChatMenuAction.rename, 'Rename', Icons.edit_outlined),
              if (canPin)
                row(
                  ChatMenuAction.pin,
                  pinned ? 'Unpin' : 'Pin',
                  pinned ? Icons.push_pin : Icons.push_pin_outlined,
                ),
              row(
                ChatMenuAction.branch,
                'Branch',
                Icons.call_split,
                disabledReason: needsIdle(),
              ),
              if (canArchive)
                row(
                  ChatMenuAction.archive,
                  'Archive',
                  Icons.archive_outlined,
                  disabledReason: needsIdle(),
                ),
            ]),
            group('Context', [
              row(
                ChatMenuAction.compress,
                'Compress',
                Icons.compress,
                disabledReason: needsLive(),
              ),
              row(
                ChatMenuAction.undo,
                'Undo last turn',
                Icons.undo,
                disabledReason:
                    needsLive() ?? (canUndo ? null : 'Nothing to undo'),
              ),
            ]),
            group('Share', [
              row(
                ChatMenuAction.export,
                'Export as Markdown',
                Icons.ios_share,
              ),
            ]),
            group('Info', [
              row(ChatMenuAction.info, 'Session info', Icons.info_outline),
            ]),
            if (canDelete)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.md),
                child: row(
                  ChatMenuAction.delete,
                  'Delete',
                  Icons.delete_outline,
                  disabledReason: needsIdle(),
                  danger: true,
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}
```

The test asserts the "Not connected yet" count is 2 (Compress and Undo) and "Stop the reply first" is 5 while replying (Branch, Archive, Compress, Undo, Delete); the rows above produce exactly those.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write Session info**

Create `lib/features/chat/session_info_sheet.dart`:

```dart
/// What is known about the open conversation (frame C3). A row with no data
/// is left out rather than filled in.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import 'relative_time.dart';

class SessionInfo {
  const SessionInfo({
    required this.title,
    required this.profile,
    this.model,
    this.lastActive,
    this.messages,
    this.contextPercent,
    this.contextUsed,
    this.contextMax,
    this.sessionId,
  });

  final String title;
  final String profile;
  final String? model;
  final DateTime? lastActive;
  final int? messages;
  final double? contextPercent;
  final int? contextUsed;
  final int? contextMax;
  final String? sessionId;
}

/// 950, 61.2k, 200k, 1.3M.
String compactTokens(int value) {
  String trim(double v) {
    final text = v.toStringAsFixed(1);
    return text.endsWith('.0') ? text.substring(0, text.length - 2) : text;
  }

  if (value >= 1000000) return '${trim(value / 1000000)}M';
  if (value >= 1000) return '${trim(value / 1000)}k';
  return '$value';
}

Future<void> showSessionInfo(BuildContext context, SessionInfo info) {
  unawaited(HapticFeedback.selectionClick());
  return showIrisSheet<void>(
    context: context,
    builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
      title: info.title,
      child: _SessionInfoBody(info: info),
    ),
  );
}

class _SessionInfoBody extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SessionInfoBody({required this.info});

  final SessionInfo info;

  String? get _context {
    final percent = info.contextPercent;
    if (percent == null) return null;
    final head = '${percent.round()}%';
    final used = info.contextUsed;
    final max = info.contextMax;
    if (used != null && max != null) {
      return '$head · ${compactTokens(used)} of ${compactTokens(max)}';
    }
    return head;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final id = info.sessionId;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        IrisSettingsGroup(
          title: 'Session',
          children: [
            IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Profile', value: info.profile),
            if (info.model case final model?)
              IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Model', value: model),
            if (info.lastActive case final at?)
              IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Last active', value: relativeTime(at, now)),
            if (info.messages case final count?)
              IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Messages', value: '$count'),
            if (_context case final usage?)
              IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Context', value: usage),
          ],
        ),
        if (id != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
          IrisLabel('Session id', color: palette.inkFaint),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: IrisSpace.md,
              vertical: IrisSpace.sm,
            ),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: palette.surfaceSunken,
              borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(IrisRadius.sm),
              border: Border.all(color: palette.line, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: SelectableText(
                    id,
                    style: type.code.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
                  ),
                ),
                IrisButton(
                  label: 'Copy',
                  tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
                  onPressed: () {
                    unawaited(Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: id)));
                    showIrisToast(context, message: 'Copied session id', top: true);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

The "Last active" test asserts `findsNothing` only when `lastActive` is null, which the conditional above guarantees.

```dart
/// What the host can do to this conversation beyond what the chat itself
/// owns. A host that passes none gets a menu without Pin, Archive and Delete.
class ChatMenuHooks {
  const ChatMenuHooks({
    required this.isPinned,
    required this.onTogglePin,
    required this.onArchive,
    required this.onDelete,
  });

  final bool Function() isPinned;
  final VoidCallback onTogglePin;

  /// Archives the conversation; true when it was archived.
  final Future<bool> Function() onArchive;

  /// Deletes it, after its own confirm; true when it was deleted.
  final Future<bool> Function() onDelete;
}
```


   Pass `onMenu: _openMenu` from `build` (`_ChatHeader(... onMenu: () => unawaited(_openMenu(state)))`).

5. Add to `_ChatScreenState`:

```dart
  Future<void> _openMenu(ChatState state) async {
    final menu = widget.menu;
    final id = _chat.conversationId;
    final title = state.title ?? 'this chat';
    final action = await showChatMenu(
      context,
      title: title,
      pinned: menu?.isPinned() ?? false,
      replying: state.running,
      live: _chat.isLive,
      canPin: menu != null && id != null,
      canArchive: menu != null && id != null && !widget.archived,
      canDelete: menu != null && id != null,
      canUndo: state.lastExchangeStartId != null,
    );
    if (!mounted || action == null) return;
    switch (action) {
      case ChatMenuAction.rename:
        final next = await showRenameSheet(context, title);
        if (next != null && mounted) await _chat.rename(next);
      case ChatMenuAction.pin:
        menu?.onTogglePin();
      case ChatMenuAction.branch:
        await _branch();
      case ChatMenuAction.archive:
        if (menu != null && await menu.onArchive() && mounted) {
          Navigator.of(context).pop();
        }
      case ChatMenuAction.compress:
        await _chat.compress();
      case ChatMenuAction.undo:
        await _chat.undo();
      case ChatMenuAction.export:
        await _export(state);
      case ChatMenuAction.info:
        await _showInfo(state);
      case ChatMenuAction.delete:
        if (menu != null && await menu.onDelete() && mounted) {
          Navigator.of(context).pop();
        }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showInfo(ChatState state) async {
    final id = _chat.conversationId;
    final summary = id == null
        ? null
        : (await widget.backend.conversation(id)).valueOrNull;
    if (!mounted) return;
    final used = state.context;
    await showSessionInfo(
      context,
      SessionInfo(
        title: state.title ?? 'New conversation',
        profile: summary?.profile ?? 'default',
        model: state.model,
        lastActive: summary?.startedAt,
        messages: summary?.messageCount,
        contextPercent: used?.percent,
        contextUsed: used?.used,
        contextMax: used?.max,
        sessionId: id,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _export(ChatState state) async {
    _toast('Export is coming in this phase');
  }
```

   `showRenameSheet` lives in `conversation_actions.dart`; import it. `_export` is replaced in Task 10; the stub toast is deliberately temporary and the commit message for this task says so.

   The profile in Session info is `summary?.profile ?? 'default'` because `ChatScreen` does not know the active profile name; for a named profile the conversation row carries it. If `conversation()` returns no profile for a named profile, pass the active profile into `ChatScreen` as a new optional `profile` parameter from the callers that have it (home's `_open`) and prefer it.

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/chat_screen.dart';

import '../support/fake_chat_backend.dart';
import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeChatBackend backend;
  setUp(() => backend = FakeChatBackend());
  tearDown(() => backend.close());

  Widget chatScreen(
    FakeChatBackend backend, {
    String conversationId = 'stored-1',
    ChatMenuHooks? menu,
  }) => themed(
    ChatScreen(
      backend: backend,
      conversationId: conversationId,
      title: 'Heartbeat gaps',
      menu: menu,
    ),
  );

  Future<void> settle(WidgetTester tester) async {
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
  }

  testWidgets('the header has a More button that opens the menu', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(chatScreen(backend));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('More'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Session info'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Rename'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('without hooks there is no Pin, Archive or Delete', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(chatScreen(backend));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('More'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Pin'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Archive'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Delete'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('Delete with hooks calls the host and closes the chat', (tester) async {
    var deleted = false;
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      chatScreen(
        backend,
        menu: ChatMenuHooks(
          isPinned: () => false,
          onTogglePin: () {},
          onArchive: () async => true,
          onDelete: () async => deleted = true,
        ),
      ),
    );
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('More'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Delete'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(deleted, isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('Compress calls the backend', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(chatScreen(backend));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('More'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Compress'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(backend.compressions, ['run-1']);
  });

  testWidgets('Session info shows the profile and the session id', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(chatScreen(backend));
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('More'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Session info'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Profile'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('stored-1'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/chat_export.dart';

void main() {
  group('transcriptMarkdown', () {
    test('headings, turns in order, and the title', () {
      final text = transcriptMarkdown('Heartbeat gaps', const [
        UserTurn(id: '1', text: 'Why the gaps?'),
        AssistantTurn(id: '2', text: 'They line up with the backup.'),
      ]);
      expect(text, startsWith('# Heartbeat gaps\n'));
      expect(text.indexOf('## You'), lessThan(text.indexOf('## Hermes')));
      expect(text, contains('Why the gaps?'));
      expect(text, contains('They line up with the backup.'));
    });

    test('attachments are listed by name', () {
      final text = transcriptMarkdown('t', const [
        UserTurn(id: '1', text: 'See these', attachments: ['a.png', 'b.pdf']),
      ]);
      expect(text, contains('_Attached: a.png, b.pdf_'));
    });

    test('a tool call becomes one quoted line, a failed one says so', () {
      final text = transcriptMarkdown('t', const [
        ToolCall(id: '1', name: 'read_logs'),
        ToolCall(id: '2', name: 'restart', status: ToolStatus.failed),
      ]);
      expect(text, contains('> Ran: `read_logs`'));
      expect(text, contains('> Tool failed: `restart`'));
    });

    test('reasoning and empty assistant turns are left out', () {
      final text = transcriptMarkdown('t', const [
        AssistantTurn(id: '1', text: '', reasoning: 'secret thoughts'),
        AssistantTurn(id: '2', text: 'Answer', reasoning: 'more thoughts'),
      ]);
      expect(text, isNot(contains('secret thoughts')));
      expect(text, isNot(contains('more thoughts')));
      expect('## Hermes'.allMatches(text), hasLength(1));
    });

    test('an error notice is included and a plain one is not', () {
      final text = transcriptMarkdown('t', const [
        Notice(id: '1', text: 'Connection lost', isError: true),
        Notice(id: '2', text: 'Stopped'),
      ]);
      expect(text, contains('> Error: Connection lost'));
      expect(text, isNot(contains('Stopped')));
    });

    test('an empty title and an empty chat still produce a document', () {
      expect(transcriptMarkdown('  ', const []), '# Chat\n');
    });
  });

  group('exportFileName', () {
    test('slugs the title', () {
      expect(exportFileName('Investigate worker heartbeat gaps'),
          'investigate-worker-heartbeat-gaps.md');
    });

    test('falls back when nothing usable is left', () {
      expect(exportFileName('???'), 'chat.md');
      expect(exportFileName(''), 'chat.md');
    });

    test('is never long', () {
      expect(exportFileName('a' * 200).length, lessThanOrEqualTo(43));
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the exporter**

Create `lib/features/chat/chat_export.dart`:

```dart
/// A conversation as a Markdown file for the system share sheet.
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:share_plus/share_plus.dart';

import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';

/// Shares [markdown] as a file. The chat screen takes one so tests do not
/// open a real share sheet.
typedef ShareMarkdown =
    Future<void> Function({
      required String fileName,
      required String title,
      required String markdown,
    });

/// The conversation as Markdown: your turns, Hermes's replies, one quoted line
/// per tool call, and errors. Reasoning is not exported.
String transcriptMarkdown(String title, List<TranscriptItem> items) {
  final heading = title.trim().isEmpty ? 'Chat' : title.trim();
  final out = StringBuffer('# $heading\n');
  for (final item in items) {
    if (item is UserTurn) {
      out.write('\n## You\n\n');
      if (item.text.trim().isNotEmpty) out.write('${item.text.trim()}\n');
      if (item.attachments.isNotEmpty) {
        out.write('\n_Attached: ${item.attachments.join(', ')}_\n');
      }
    } else if (item is AssistantTurn) {
      if (item.text.trim().isEmpty) continue;
      out.write('\n## Hermes\n\n${item.text.trim()}\n');
    } else if (item is ToolCall) {
      final verb = item.status == ToolStatus.failed ? 'Tool failed' : 'Ran';
      out.write('\n> $verb: `${item.name}`\n');
    } else if (item is Notice) {
      if (item.isError) out.write('\n> Error: ${item.text.trim()}\n');
    }
  }
  return out.toString();
}

/// `Investigate heartbeat gaps` as `investigate-heartbeat-gaps.md`.
String exportFileName(String title) {
  final slug = title
      .toLowerCase()
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]+'), '-')
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'^-+|-+$'), '');
  final short = slug.length > 40
      ? slug.substring(0, 40).replaceAll(RegExp(r'-+$'), '')
      : slug;
  return '${short.isEmpty ? 'chat' : short}.md';
}

Future<void> shareMarkdownFile({
  required String fileName,
  required String title,
  required String markdown,
}) async {
  final directory = await getTemporaryDirectory();
  final file = File('${directory.path}/$fileName');
  await file.writeAsString(markdown);
  await SharePlus.instance.share(
    ShareParams(
      files: [XFile(file.path, mimeType: 'text/markdown')],
      subject: title,
    ),
  );
}
```

```dart
  /// Every message of the conversation, oldest first: reads the pages not yet
  /// loaded. Stops at the first page that fails to load.
  Future<List<TranscriptItem>> fullTranscript() async {
    for (var i = 0; i < 200 && !_disposed && value.earlier != null; i++) {
      final before = value.earlier;
      await loadEarlier();
      if (identical(value.earlier, before)) break;
    }
    return value.items;
  }
```

Create `lib/features/profiles/delete_profile_sheet.dart`:

```dart
/// Deleting a profile asks for its name, because it removes data from the
/// server (frame D4).
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';

/// True when the person typed the name exactly and confirmed.
Future<bool> showDeleteProfileSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String name,
  required String host,
}) async {
  final confirmed = await showIrisSheet<bool>(
    context: context,
    builder: (sheetContext) => _DeleteProfileBody(name: name, host: host),
  );
  return confirmed ?? false;
}

class _DeleteProfileBody extends StatefulWidget {
  const _DeleteProfileBody({required this.name, required this.host});

  final String name;
  final String host;

  @override
  State<_DeleteProfileBody> createState() => _DeleteProfileBodyState();
}

class _DeleteProfileBodyState extends State<_DeleteProfileBody> {
  final _typed = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _typed.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _typed.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => IrisSheet(
    title: 'Delete ${widget.name}?',
    subtitle:
        'Its sessions, memory, skills and scheduled jobs are removed from '
        "${widget.host}. This can't be undone.",
    actions: IrisSheetActions(
      secondary: IrisButton(
        label: 'Cancel',
        onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
      ),
      primary: IrisButton(
        label: 'Delete',
        tone: IrisButtonTone.danger,
        onPressed: _typed.text == widget.name
            ? () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true)
            : null,
      ),
    ),
    child: IrisField(
      label: 'Type ${widget.name} to confirm',
      controller: _typed,
      mono: true,
      autofocus: true,
      autocorrect: false,
      enableSuggestions: false,
    ),
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the form**

Create `lib/features/profiles/profile_form_screen.dart`:

```dart
/// A new profile, or an existing one's name and SOUL (frames D2, D3).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/automation_providers.dart';
import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/components/settings_list.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/components/toast.dart';
import '../../design/platform/adaptive.dart';
import '../../design/platform/iris_scaffold.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../automation_errors.dart';
import 'delete_profile_sheet.dart';

class ProfileFormScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ProfileFormScreen({this.editing, super.key});

  /// The profile being edited; null creates one.
  final String? editing;

  @override
  ConsumerState<ProfileFormScreen> createState() => _ProfileFormScreenState();
}

class _ProfileFormScreenState extends ConsumerState<ProfileFormScreen> {
  late final _name = TextEditingController(text: widget.editing ?? '');
  final _soul = TextEditingController();
  ProfileStart _start = ProfileStart.blank;
  String _source = 'default';
  bool _mono = true;
  bool _saving = false;
  String? _nameError;

  /// The SOUL as the server holds it; null until loaded, or if it could not
  /// be, so a failed read never turns into an empty overwrite.
  String? _loadedSoul;

  bool get _editing => widget.editing != null;
  bool get _isDefault => widget.editing == 'default';

  bool get _nameChanged => _editing && !_isDefault && _name.text != widget.editing;
  bool get _soulChanged => _loadedSoul != null && _soul.text != _loadedSoul;

  bool get _canSave {
    if (_saving) return false;
    if (!_editing) return ProfileName.isValid(_name.text);
    if (_nameChanged && !ProfileName.isValid(_name.text)) return false;
    return _nameChanged || _soulChanged;
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _name.addListener(() => setState(() => _nameError = null));
    _soul.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
    if (_editing) unawaited(_loadSoul());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _name.dispose();
    _soul.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSoul() async {
    final repository = await ref.read(profileRepositoryProvider.future);
    final result = await repository?.soul(widget.editing!);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (result case Ok(:final value)) {
      setState(() {
        _loadedSoul = value;
        _soul.text = value;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickSource() async {
    final profiles = ref.read(profilesProvider).value ?? const <AgentProfile>[];
    final picked = await showIrisSheet<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (sheetContext) => IrisSheet(
        title: 'Copy from',
        child: Column(
          children: [
            for (final profile in profiles)
              IrisListRow(
                title: profile.name,
                selected: profile.name == _source,
                onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(profile.name),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (picked != null && mounted) setState(() => _source = picked);
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final repository = await ref.read(profileRepositoryProvider.future);
    if (repository == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    Result<void> result;
    var name = widget.editing ?? _name.text;
    if (!_editing) {
      result = await repository.create(
        ProfileDraft(
          name: _name.text,
          start: _start,
          source: _source,
          soul: _soul.text,
        ),
      );
    } else {
      result = const Ok(null);
      if (_nameChanged) {
        result = await repository.rename(widget.editing!, _name.text);
        if (result.isOk) {
          name = _name.text;
          // The profile on screen follows its new name.
          if (ref.read(activeProfileProvider) == widget.editing) {
            ref.read(activeProfileProvider.notifier).select(name);
          }
        }
      }
      if (result.isOk && _soulChanged) {
        result = await repository.setSoul(name, _soul.text);
      }
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        ref.invalidate(profilesProvider);
        Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
      case Err(:final failure):
        setState(() => _saving = false);
        if (!_editing &&
            failure.kind == FailureKind.rejected &&
            failure.message != null) {
          setState(() => _nameError = failure.message);
        } else {
          showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
        }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _delete() async {
    final name = widget.editing!;
    final host =
        Uri.tryParse(ref.read(activeServerProvider)?.baseUrl ?? '')?.host ??
        'this server';
    if (!await showDeleteProfileSheet(context, name: name, host: host) ||
        !mounted) {
      return;
    }
    final repository = await ref.read(profileRepositoryProvider.future);
    final result = await repository?.delete(name);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      showIrisToast(context, message: describeWrite(failure), top: true);
      return;
    }
    // A deleted profile cannot stay selected.
    if (ref.read(activeProfileProvider) == name) {
      ref.read(activeProfileProvider.notifier).select('default');
    }
    ref
      ..invalidate(profilesProvider)
      ..invalidate(conversationsProvider);
    Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
    showIrisToast(context, message: 'Deleted ‘$name’');
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final gutter = context.window.gutter;
    final profiles = ref.watch(profilesProvider).value ?? const <AgentProfile>[];
    final model = profiles
        .where((p) => p.name == widget.editing)
        .map((p) => p.model)
        .firstOrNull;
    final nameProblem = _nameError ?? ProfileName.problem(_name.text);

    return IrisScaffold(
      header: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.sm, gutter, IrisSpace.sm),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            IrisButton(
              label: 'Cancel',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost,
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                _editing ? 'Edit profile' : 'New profile',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: type.heading.copyWith(color: palette.ink),
              ),
            ),
            IrisButton(
              label: _editing ? 'Save' : 'Create',
              tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
              busy: _saving,
              onPressed: _canSave ? () => unawaited(_save()) : null,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.lg, gutter, IrisSpace.xxl),
        children: [
          if (_isDefault)
            IrisSettingsGroup(
              title: 'Profile',
              children: [IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Name', value: 'default')],
            )
          else ...[
            IrisField(
              label: 'Name',
              hint: 'work-notes',
              controller: _name,
              mono: true,
              autocorrect: false,
              enableSuggestions: false,
              errorText: nameProblem,
            ),
            if (nameProblem == null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              Text(
                'Lowercase letters, digits, - and _ only',
                style: type.caption.copyWith(color: palette.inkFaint),
              ),
            ],
          ],
          if (_editing) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            IrisSettingsGroup(
              title: 'From config',
              children: [IrisSettingsRow(label: 'Model', value: model ?? '—')],
            ),
          ] else ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
            IrisSettingsGroup(
              title: 'Start from',
              children: [
                _StartRow(
                  label: 'Blank',
                  sub: 'Nothing copied',
                  selected: _start == ProfileStart.blank,
                  onTap: () => setState(() => _start = ProfileStart.blank),
                ),
                _StartRow(
                  label: 'Copy',
                  sub: 'Copies config and skills',
                  selected: _start == ProfileStart.copy,
                  onTap: () => setState(() => _start = ProfileStart.copy),
                ),
                _StartRow(
                  label: 'Full copy',
                  sub: 'Also copies memory and sessions',
                  selected: _start == ProfileStart.full,
                  onTap: () => setState(() => _start = ProfileStart.full),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (_start != ProfileStart.blank) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              IrisSettingsGroup(
                title: 'Source',
                children: [
                  IrisSettingsRow(
                    label: 'Copy from',
                    value: _source,
                    onTap: () => unawaited(_pickSource()),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xl),
          IrisField(
            label: _editing ? 'SOUL.md' : 'SOUL · optional',
            hint: 'Who this profile is and how it should work. Markdown.',
            controller: _soul,
            mono: _mono,
            minLines: 6,
            maxLines: 16,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
```

Create `lib/data/chat/search_merge.dart`:

```dart
/// Putting the hits of several profiles' searches into one list.
library;

import 'chat_models.dart';

/// Each hit labelled with its profile, all of them newest first. A hit with no
/// time sorts after every dated one, and ties keep the order they came in.
List<SearchHit> mergeProfileHits(Map<String, List<SearchHit>> byProfile) {
  final labelled = <SearchHit>[
    for (final entry in byProfile.entries)
      for (final hit in entry.value) hit.withProfile(entry.key),
  ];
  final indexed = labelled.indexed.toList()
    ..sort((a, b) {
      final x = a.$2.lastActive;
      final y = b.$2.lastActive;
      if (x == null && y == null) return a.$1.compareTo(b.$1);
      if (x == null) return 1;
      if (y == null) return -1;
      final byTime = y.compareTo(x);
      return byTime != 0 ? byTime : a.$1.compareTo(b.$1);
    });
  return [for (final (_, hit) in indexed) hit];
}
```

```dart
  /// A search across [profiles], hits labelled by profile and merged. Fails
  /// only when every profile's search fails.
  Future<Result<List<SearchHit>>> searchAcross(
    String query,
    List<String> profiles,
  );
```

Implement in `DashboardChat`:

```dart
  @override
  Future<Result<List<SearchHit>>> searchAcross(
    String query,
    List<String> profiles,
  ) async {
    final results = await Future.wait([
      for (final name in profiles)
        _session.connection.client
            .get(
              '/api/sessions/search',
              query: {'q': query, 'limit': searchLimit, 'profile': name},
            )
            .then((result) => (name, result.map(UsageParser.searchHits))),
    ]);
    final byProfile = <String, List<SearchHit>>{
      for (final (name, result) in results)
        if (result.valueOrNull case final hits?) name: hits,
    };
    if (byProfile.isEmpty && results.isNotEmpty) {
      return Err(results.first.$2.failureOrNull!);
    }
    return Ok(mergeProfileHits(byProfile));
  }
```

```dart
        asked.add(profile);
        return FakeReply.json({
          'results': [
            {'session_id': '$profile-1', 'title': 'Deploy in $profile'},
          ],
        });
      });
      final hits = (await chat.searchAcross('deploy', ['default', 'research']))
          .valueOrNull!;
      expect(asked.toSet(), {'default', 'research'});
      expect({for (final h in hits) h.storedId: h.profile}, {
        'default-1': 'default',
        'research-1': 'research',
      });
    });

    test('searchAcross survives one profile failing', () async {
      http.on('GET', '/api/sessions/search', (request) {
        return request.uri.queryParameters['profile'] == 'gone'
            ? FakeReply.json({'detail': 'Not Found'}, status: 404)
            : FakeReply.json({
                'results': [
                  {'session_id': 'a', 'title': 'A'},
                ],
              });
      });
      final result = await chat.searchAcross('x', ['default', 'gone']);
      expect(result.valueOrNull!.single.profile, 'default');
    });

    test('searchAcross fails when every profile fails', () async {
      final result = await chat.searchAcross('x', ['default', 'gone']);
      expect(result.isErr, isTrue);
    });
```

In `lib/features/chat/conversation_search.dart` add imports for `segmented.dart` and `chat_models` (already), then:

```dart
/// What a home search covers.
enum SearchScope {
  here('This profile'),
  all('All profiles');

  const SearchScope(this.label);
  final String label;
}

final searchScopeProvider = NotifierProvider<SearchScopeState, SearchScope>(
  SearchScopeState.new,
);

class SearchScopeState extends Notifier<SearchScope> {
  @override
  SearchScope build() => SearchScope.here;

  void select(SearchScope scope) => state = scope;
}

/// The hits for a query across every profile on the server, labelled by
/// profile.
final crossProfileSearchProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose
    .family<List<SearchHit>, String>((ref, query) async {
      final chat = await ref.watch(activeChatProvider.future);
      if (chat == null) return const [];
      final names = [
        for (final profile in ref.watch(profilesProvider).value ?? const [])
          profile.name,
      ];
      final profiles = names.isEmpty ? [ref.read(activeProfileProvider)] : names;
      return switch (await chat.searchAcross(query, profiles)) {
        Ok(:final value) => value,
        Err(:final failure) => throw failure,
      };
    });

/// "This profile | All profiles", under the search field.
class SearchScopeBar extends StatelessWidget {
  const SearchScopeBar({required this.scope, required this.onChanged, super.key});

  final SearchScope scope;
  final ValueChanged<SearchScope> onChanged;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => IrisSegmented<SearchScope>(
    values: SearchScope.values,
    selected: scope,
    labelOf: (value) => value.label,
    onChanged: onChanged,
  );
}
```

4. Add the method:

```dart
  /// Opens a search hit. A hit under another profile switches to that profile
  /// first, and opens through the backend that profile's switch builds: the old
  /// backend belongs to the profile being left.
  Future<void> _openHit(
    ChatBackend current,
    SearchHit hit,
    ConversationSummary conversation,
  ) async {
    final target = hit.profile;
    if (target == null || target == ref.read(activeProfileProvider)) {
      await _open(current, conversation: conversation);
      return;
    }
    ref.read(activeProfileProvider.notifier).select(target);
    final next = await ref.read(activeChatProvider.future);
    if (!mounted || next == null) return;
    _search.clear();
    await _open(next, conversation: conversation);
  }
```

Replace the `: <Widget>[ for (final (index, found) in shown.indexed) Builder(...) ]` branch with a call to a new method, so the headers can be tracked in an ordinary loop:


     and add the method to `_ConversationList`:

```dart
  /// One row per hit, with a profile heading before each profile's first hit
  /// when the search covers every profile.
  List<Widget> _hitRows(
    BuildContext context,
    double gutter,
    List<SearchHit> shown,
  ) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final rows = <Widget>[];
    String? lastProfile;
    for (final (index, found) in shown.indexed) {
      if (scope == SearchScope.all &&
          found.profile != null &&
          found.profile != lastProfile) {
        rows.add(
          Padding(
            key: ValueKey('heading-${found.profile}'),
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, IrisSpace.md, gutter, IrisSpace.xs),
            child: IrisLabel(found.profile!, color: palette.inkFaint),
          ),
        );
      }
      lastProfile = found.profile;
      final title = titles[found.storedId] ?? found.title;
      rows.add(
        Padding(
          key: ValueKey('hit-${found.profile}-${found.storedId}'),
          padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(gutter, 0, gutter, IrisSpace.xs),
          child: SearchHitRow(
            query: search.text,
            now: now,
            best: index == 0,
            pinned: pins.contains(found.storedId),
            hit: SearchHit(
              storedId: found.storedId,
              title: title,
              snippet: found.snippet,
              lastActive: found.lastActive,
              messageCount: found.messageCount,
              matches: found.matches,
              profile: found.profile,
            ),
            onTap: onOpenHit == null
                ? null
                : () => onOpenHit!(
                    found,
                    ConversationSummary(
                      id: found.storedId,
                      title: title,
                      profile: found.profile,
                    ),
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return rows;
  }
```

     The old per-hit `Builder` and its `onTap: open == null ? null : () => open(...)` are deleted; the `open` parameter of `_searchItems` stays for the local-match rows.

```dart
                ? 'Searched ${profileNames.join(', ')}.'
                : 'Searching $currentProfile only. Switch to All profiles to '

```

```dart
      expect(jobs, hasLength(4));
      expect(jobs.where((j) => j.lastError != null), hasLength(1));
      expect(jobs.where((j) => !j.enabled), hasLength(1));
      expect(jobs.every((j) => j.title.isNotEmpty), isTrue);
    });

    test('pause and resume change the job', () async {
      final id = (await demo.cron.jobs()).valueOrNull!.first.id;

        (await demo.cron.jobs()).valueOrNull!.firstWhere((j) => j.id == id).enabled,
        isFalse,
      );
    });

    test('create, edit and delete', () async {
      final created = (await demo.cron.create(
        const JobDraft(schedule: 'every 2h', prompt: 'Check', name: 'Check'),
      )).valueOrNull!;
      expect((await demo.cron.jobs()).valueOrNull!.map((j) => j.id), contains(created.id));
      await demo.cron.update(
        created.id,
        const JobDraft(schedule: '30m', prompt: 'Check again', name: 'Check'),
      );
      expect(
        (await demo.cron.job(created.id)).valueOrNull!.schedule,
        '30m',
      );
      await demo.cron.delete(created.id);
      expect((await demo.cron.jobs()).valueOrNull!.map((j) => j.id),
          isNot(contains(created.id)));
    });

    test('an unparseable schedule is refused like the server would', () async {

      );
      expect(result.failureOrNull?.message, contains("Couldn't parse"));
    });

    test('running a job adds a past run that opens', () async {
      final id = (await demo.cron.jobs()).valueOrNull!.first.id;
      await demo.cron.trigger(id);

      await demo.kanban.move(todo.id, 'done');
      expect((await demo.kanban.board()).valueOrNull!.task(todo.id)!.status, 'done');
      final locked = await demo.kanban.move(todo.id, 'running');
      expect(locked.isErr, isTrue);
      final created = (await demo.kanban.create(
```

In `lib/demo/demo_world.dart`, replace `List<AgentProfile> get profiles => demoProfiles;` with:

```dart
  final List<AgentProfile> _profiles = [...demoProfiles];
  final Map<String, String> _souls = {
    'default': '# Default\n\nYou are Iris’s demo agent.',
  };

  List<AgentProfile> get profiles => List.unmodifiable(_profiles);

  String soulOf(String name) => _souls[name] ?? '';

  void setSoul(String name, String soul) => _souls[name] = soul;

  /// A profile made in the demo. [copyFrom] gives it that profile's model.
  void addProfile(String name, {String? copyFrom, String? soul}) {
    final source = _profiles.where((p) => p.name == copyFrom).firstOrNull;
    _profiles.add(
      AgentProfile(name: name, model: source?.model, provider: source?.provider),
    );
    if (soul != null && soul.isNotEmpty) _souls[name] = soul;
  }

  void renameProfile(String name, String next) {
    final at = _profiles.indexWhere((p) => p.name == name);
    if (at < 0) return;
    final old = _profiles[at];
    _profiles[at] = AgentProfile(
      name: next,
      isDefault: old.isDefault,
      model: old.model,
      provider: old.provider,
    );
    if (_souls.containsKey(name)) _souls[next] = _souls.remove(name)!;
  }

  void removeProfile(String name) {
    _profiles.removeWhere((p) => p.name == name && !p.isDefault);
    _souls.remove(name);
  }
```

Run `flutter test test/demo` and fix any test that assumed two profiles (`demo_world_test.dart:198` only checks the result type).

Create `lib/demo/demo_automation.dart`:

```dart
/// The Scheduled, Tasks and Profiles tools in the demo: real-looking data held
/// in memory, so a reviewer can use every screen without a server.
library;

import '../core/failure.dart';
import '../core/result.dart';
import '../data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import '../data/automation/cron_repository.dart';
import '../data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import '../data/automation/kanban_repository.dart';
import '../data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import '../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'demo_world.dart';

final class DemoAutomation {
  DemoAutomation(this._world, {DateTime Function() clock = DateTime.now})
    : _clock = clock {
    final now = clock();
    _jobs.addAll([
      CronJob(
        id: 'job-1',
        name: 'Morning inbox digest',
        prompt: 'Summarise what needs a reply from overnight.',
        schedule: '0 7 * * *',
        scheduleDisplay: 'every day 7am',
        enabled: true,
        state: CronState.active,
        nextRun: now.add(const Duration(hours: 9, minutes: 12)),
        model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5',
      ),
      CronJob(
        id: 'job-2',
        name: 'Nightly backup to cold storage',
        prompt: 'Back up /srv/data to the s3-cold bucket.\nVerify the checksum.',
        schedule: '0 2 * * *',
        enabled: true,
        state: CronState.active,
        lastRun: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 2)),
        nextRun: now.add(const Duration(hours: 21, minutes: 40)),
        lastError: 'backup target returned 502 Bad Gateway (s3-cold, 3 retries)',
        deliver: 'telegram-ops',
      ),
      CronJob(
        id: 'job-3',
        prompt: 'Audit dependencies for known advisories.',
        schedule: '0 9 * * 1',
        scheduleDisplay: 'every monday 9am',
        enabled: true,
        state: CronState.active,
        nextRun: now.add(const Duration(days: 3, hours: 4)),
      ),
      CronJob(
        id: 'job-4',
        name: 'Sync Linear → Tasks',
        prompt: 'Mirror new Linear issues into the task board.',
        schedule: '*/15 * * * *',
        enabled: false,
        state: CronState.paused,
      ),
    ]);
    _tasks.addAll(_seedTasks(now));
    _comments['t_26'] = [
      KanbanComment(
        author: 'you',
        body: 'Hold off until the 14:00 deploy is out.',
        at: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 2)),
      ),
      KanbanComment(
        author: 'homelab',
        body: 'Deploy is finished, starting the rotation now.',
        at: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1)),
      ),
    ];
  }

  final DemoWorld _world;
  final DateTime Function() _clock;
  final List<CronJob> _jobs = [];
  final Map<String, List<ConversationSummary>> _runs = {};
  final List<KanbanTask> _tasks = [];
  final Map<String, List<KanbanComment>> _comments = {};
  var _jobCounter = 4;
  var _taskCounter = 40;

  late final CronRepository cron = _DemoCron(this);
  late final KanbanRepository kanban = _DemoKanban(this);
  late final ProfileRepository profiles = _DemoProfiles(_world);

  static const _columns = [
    'triage',
    'todo',
    'ready',
    'running',
    'blocked',
    'review',
    'done',
  ];

  static List<KanbanTask> _seedTasks(DateTime now) => [
    KanbanTask(id: 't_29', title: 'Flaky iOS UI tests on CI', status: 'triage'),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_27',
      title: 'Draft Q3 roadmap summary for the board',
      status: 'todo',
      priority: 2,
    ),
    KanbanTask(id: 't_24', title: 'Rotate gateway API keys', status: 'todo'),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_33',
      title: 'Write passkey migration notes',
      status: 'ready',
      assignee: 'homelab',
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_30',
      title: 'Investigate worker heartbeat gaps overnight',
      status: 'running',
      assignee: 'homelab',
      priority: 2,
      latestSummary: 'Pulled the heartbeat logs between 01:00 and 05:00.',
      startedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(minutes: 4)),
      progress: const KanbanProgress(2, 5),
      commentCount: 1,
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_28',
      title: 'Refactor billing to usage-based pricing',
      status: 'running',
      assignee: 'research',
      startedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(minutes: 11)),
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_31',
      title: 'Nightly backup failing with 502 from gateway',
      status: 'blocked',
      blockKind: 'needs_input',
      latestSummary: 'Needs a decision: retry, or move the job to 04:30.',
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_35',
      title: 'Update billing-worker',
      status: 'blocked',
      blockKind: 'dependency',
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_36',
      title: 'Update webhook-relay',
      status: 'blocked',
      blockKind: 'capability',
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_37',
      title: 'Sync the model catalogue',
      status: 'blocked',
      blockKind: 'transient',
      warningCount: 1,
    ),
    KanbanTask(
      id: 't_26',
      title: 'Rotate gateway API keys',
      status: 'review',
      assignee: 'homelab',
      priority: 2,
      latestSummary: 'Rotated keys and restarted all three services.',
      commentCount: 2,
    ),
    KanbanTask(id: 't_22', title: 'Local embeddings vs API cost, report', status: 'done'),
  ];

  KanbanBoard _board() => KanbanBoard(
    columns: [
      for (final id in _columns)
        KanbanColumn(
          id: id,
          tasks: [
            for (final task in _tasks)
              if (task.status == id) task,
          ],
        ),
    ],
  );

  static final _knownSchedule = RegExp(
    r'^(\d+[smhd]|every\s+.+|([\d*/,-]+\s+){4}[\d*/,\-A-Za-z]+|\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}.*)$',
    caseSensitive: false,
  );

  static IrisFailure? _scheduleProblem(String schedule) {
    final text = schedule.trim();
    final knownWords = RegExp(
      r'^every\s+(\d+\s*)?(second|minute|hour|day|week|month|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)s?(\s+.*)?$',
      caseSensitive: false,
    );
    final ok =
        _knownSchedule.hasMatch(text) &&
        (!text.toLowerCase().startsWith('every') || knownWords.hasMatch(text));
    return ok
        ? null
        : IrisFailure(
            FailureKind.rejected,
            statusCode: 422,
            message: "Couldn't parse '$text'",
          );
  }
}

final class _DemoCron implements CronRepository {
  _DemoCron(this._demo);
  final DemoAutomation _demo;

  List<CronJob> get _jobs => _demo._jobs;

  int _index(String id) => _jobs.indexWhere((j) => j.id == id);

  @override
  Future<Result<List<CronJob>>> jobs() async => Ok(List.of(_jobs));

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> job(String id) async => _index(id) < 0
      ? const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound))
      : Ok(_jobs[_index(id)]);

  @override
  Future<Result<List<ConversationSummary>>> runs(String id, {int limit = 20}) async =>
      Ok(List.of(_demo._runs[id] ?? const []));

  @override
  Future<Result<List<DeliveryTarget>>> deliveryTargets() async => const Ok([
    DeliveryTarget(id: 'telegram-ops', name: 'telegram-ops'),
    DeliveryTarget(id: 'email-me', name: 'email'),
  ]);

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> create(JobDraft draft) async {
    final problem = DemoAutomation._scheduleProblem(draft.schedule);
    if (problem != null) return Err(problem);
    final job = CronJob(
      id: 'job-${++_demo._jobCounter}',
      name: draft.name?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : draft.name!.trim(),
      prompt: draft.prompt.trim(),
      schedule: draft.schedule.trim(),
      enabled: true,
      state: CronState.active,
      nextRun: _demo._clock().add(const Duration(hours: 1)),
      model: draft.model,
      deliver: draft.deliver,
    );
    _jobs.add(job);
    return Ok(job);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> update(String id, JobDraft draft) async {
    final at = _index(id);
    if (at < 0) return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    final problem = DemoAutomation._scheduleProblem(draft.schedule);
    if (problem != null) return Err(problem);
    final old = _jobs[at];
    return Ok(
      _jobs[at] = CronJob(
        id: id,
        name: draft.name?.trim().isEmpty ?? true ? null : draft.name!.trim(),
        prompt: draft.prompt.trim(),
        schedule: draft.schedule.trim(),
        enabled: old.enabled,
        state: old.state,
        nextRun: old.nextRun,
        lastRun: old.lastRun,
        lastError: old.lastError,
        model: draft.model,
        deliver: draft.deliver,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<CronJob>> setEnabled(String id, {required bool enabled}) async {
    final at = _index(id);
    if (at < 0) return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    return Ok(_jobs[at] = _jobs[at].copyWith(enabled: enabled));
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> trigger(String id) async {
    final at = _index(id);
    if (at < 0) return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    final created = _demo._world.create('default');
    final now = _demo._clock();
    (_demo._runs[id] ??= []).insert(
      0,
      ConversationSummary(
        id: created.storedId,
        title: _jobs[at].title,
        startedAt: now,
        source: 'cron',
      ),
    );
    return const Ok(null);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String id) async {
    _jobs.removeWhere((j) => j.id == id);
    _demo._runs.remove(id);
    return const Ok(null);
  }
}

final class _DemoKanban implements KanbanRepository {
  _DemoKanban(this._demo);
  final DemoAutomation _demo;

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanBoard>> board() async => Ok(_demo._board());

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTaskDetail>> task(String id) async {
    final task = _demo._tasks.where((t) => t.id == id).firstOrNull;
    if (task == null) return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    final now = _demo._clock();
    return Ok(
      KanbanTaskDetail(
        task: task,
        result: id == 't_26' ? 'New keys are live in vault/gateway. Old keys were revoked at 14:20.' : null,
        lastFailure: id == 't_26' ? 'webhook-relay: health check timed out after 30s' : null,
        comments: _demo._comments[id] ?? const [],
        runs: id == 't_26'
            ? [
                KanbanRun(
                  startedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1)),
                  endedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(minutes: 40)),
                  outcome: 'completed',
                ),
                KanbanRun(
                  startedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 2)),
                  endedAt: now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1, minutes: 50)),
                  outcome: 'failed',
                ),
              ]
            : const [],
        parents: id == 't_26'
            ? const [KanbanLink(id: 't_20', title: 'Q4 security hygiene')]
            : const [],
        children: id == 't_26'
            ? const [
                KanbanLink(id: 't_34', title: 'Update api-edge', status: 'done'),
                KanbanLink(id: 't_35', title: 'Update billing-worker', status: 'blocked'),
                KanbanLink(id: 't_36', title: 'Update webhook-relay', status: 'blocked'),
              ]
            : const [],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<KanbanTask?>> create(TaskDraft draft) async {
    final task = KanbanTask(
      id: 't_${++_demo._taskCounter}',
      title: draft.title.trim(),
      status: draft.status,
      body: draft.body,
      assignee: draft.assignee,
      priority: draft.priority,
    );
    _demo._tasks.add(task);
    return Ok(task);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> move(String id, String status) async {
    if (KanbanColumns.isSystemSet(status)) {
      return const Err(
        IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'Hermes moves tasks there'),
      );
    }
    final at = _demo._tasks.indexWhere((t) => t.id == id);
    if (at < 0) return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.notFound));
    _demo._tasks[at] = _demo._tasks[at].copyWith(status: status);
    return const Ok(null);
  }
}

final class _DemoProfiles implements ProfileRepository {
  _DemoProfiles(this._world);
  final DemoWorld _world;

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> create(ProfileDraft draft) async {
    if (_world.profiles.any((p) => p.name == draft.name)) {
      return const Err(
        IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'That profile already exists'),
      );
    }
    _world.addProfile(
      draft.name,
      copyFrom: draft.start == ProfileStart.blank ? null : draft.source,
      soul: draft.soul,
    );
    return const Ok(null);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> rename(String name, String newName) async {
    _world.renameProfile(name, newName);
    return const Ok(null);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<String>> soul(String name) async => Ok(_world.soulOf(name));

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setSoul(String name, String content) async {
    _world.setSoul(name, content);
    return const Ok(null);
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String name) async {
    if (name == 'default') {
      return const Err(
        IrisFailure(
          FailureKind.rejected,
          message: 'The default profile cannot be deleted.',
        ),
      );
    }
    _world.removeProfile(name);
    return const Ok(null);
  }
}
```

`_DemoCron.trigger` assumes `DemoWorld.create(String profile)` returns a `NewConversation` with `storedId` (it does: `chat.create()` returns `Ok(_world.create(profile))`). The `_DemoKanban.task` method returns a detail even for tasks with no seeded extras, which is intended.
