### Task 15: Live write test

**Files:**
- Create: `test/live/phase7_write_live_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the three `Dashboard*Repository` classes, a live `ApiClient` as in the probe, `~/.iris-live.json`.

This test writes to the user's real server, so it only touches things it creates itself: a throwaway profile named `iris-p7-<timestamp>`, a job inside it, and a task it creates in Triage. It never touches the default profile's jobs. Skip any step whose route the probe note says is missing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `test/live/phase7_write_live_test.dart`:

```dart
/// Writes to the real server: a throwaway profile, a job in it, a task.
///   flutter test --tags live --run-skipped test/live/phase7_write_live_test.dart
@Tags(['live'])
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/cron_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/kanban_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/automation/profile_repository.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/api_client.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/credentials.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/dashboard_session.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/rpc_frame.dart';

void main() {
  late DashboardSession session;
  late ApiClient api;

  setUp(() async {
    final json = Json.decode(
      File(
        '${Platform.environment['USERPROFILE'] ?? Platform.environment['HOME']}'
        '/.iris-live.json',
      ).readAsStringSync(),
    );
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      ServerConfig(
        id: 'live',
        baseUrl: ServerConfig.normaliseBaseUrl(json['base_url'].string()),
        label: 'live',
      ),
      secrets: ServerSecrets(
        username: json['username'].stringOrNull,
        password: json['password'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientId: json['cf_client_id'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientSecret: json['cf_client_secret'].stringOrNull,
      ),
    );
    session = DashboardSession(http: connection);
    addTearDown(() async {
      await session.dispose();
      connection.close();
    });
    await session.start();
    expect(session.state.phase, LinkPhase.live);
    api = connection.client;
  });

  test(
    'profile, job and task round trip',
    () async {
      final stamp = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
      final name = 'iris-p7-$stamp';
      final renamed = 'iris-p7r-$stamp';
      final profiles = DashboardProfileRepository(api);
      final kanban = DashboardKanbanRepository(api);

      // Profile: create, read the SOUL, rename, write the SOUL.
      expect(
        (await profiles.create(ProfileDraft(name: name, soul: '# Probe'))).isOk,
        isTrue,
      );
      addTearDown(() async {
        // Whatever happened below, the throwaway profile goes.
        await profiles.delete(renamed);
        await profiles.delete(name);
      });
      expect((await profiles.soul(name)).valueOrNull, contains('Probe'));
      expect((await profiles.rename(name, renamed)).isOk, isTrue);
      expect((await profiles.setSoul(renamed, '# Probe 2')).isOk, isTrue);

      // A job in that profile: create, pause, resume, run, delete.
      final cron = DashboardCronRepository(api, profile: renamed);
      final job = (await cron.create(
        const JobDraft(
          schedule: 'every 6h',
          prompt: 'Reply with the word: ok',
          name: 'Iris probe',
        ),
      )).valueOrNull;
      expect(job, isNotNull);
      expect(
        (await cron.setEnabled(job!.id, enabled: false)).valueOrNull!.enabled,
        isFalse,
      );
      expect(
        (await cron.setEnabled(job.id, enabled: true)).valueOrNull!.enabled,
        isTrue,
      );
      expect((await cron.trigger(job.id)).isOk, isTrue);
      // Replies are slow (about 2.5 minutes): wait for the run to appear.
      var runs = (await cron.runs(job.id)).valueOrNull ?? const [];
      for (var i = 0; i < 24 && runs.isEmpty; i++) {
        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 10));
        runs = (await cron.runs(job.id)).valueOrNull ?? const [];
      }
      expect(runs, isNotEmpty, reason: 'the triggered run never appeared');
      expect((await cron.delete(job.id)).isOk, isTrue);

      // A task: create in Triage, move to To do. There is no delete route in
      // scope, so the task stays; it is named so it can be cleared by hand.
      final task = (await kanban.create(
        TaskDraft(title: 'Iris probe $stamp', status: 'triage'),
      )).valueOrNull;
      if (task != null) {
        expect((await kanban.move(task.id, 'todo')).isOk, isTrue);
        final board = (await kanban.board()).valueOrNull!;
        expect(board.task(task.id)?.status, 'todo');
      }

      // The teardown deletes the profile; check that it really goes.
      expect((await profiles.delete(renamed)).isOk, isTrue);
    },
    timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 8)),
  );
}
```

The probe's `setUp` is repeated rather than shared, as the Phase 5 and 6 live tests do. The leftover Kanban task is named `Iris probe <stamp>`; tell the user so it can be cleared from the desktop app.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

Run: `flutter test --tags live --run-skipped test/live/phase7_write_live_test.dart`
Expected: PASS in about 3–6 minutes. If a step fails because the probe showed that route missing, comment that step out with the probe note's finding as the reason, not silently.

- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**

```bash
git add test/live/phase7_write_live_test.dart
git commit -m "test(phase7): a live write test on a throwaway profile"
```

```markdown
# Phase 7 device pass — <date>

Build: release APK, commit <sha>. Emulator: Pixel_9, <dark / light>.

## Demo (no server needed)
- Welcome → Try the demo: home shows Sessions | Scheduled | Tasks — 
- Scheduled: four jobs; failed one in red; paused one off; the new-job button — 
- Pause and resume a job; the switch moves at once — 
- Job detail: Run now says Started; a past run appears and opens — 
- New job: a bad schedule ("every blursday") shows the message under Schedule — 
- Edit a job; Delete asks first — 
- Tasks: chips with counts; Running card has the clock and "2 of 5 subtasks"; Blocked cards show each kind — 
- An empty Review column says Hermes moves tasks there — 
- Task detail t_26: parent link, subtasks, comments, runs; Move to Done; Running is locked — 
- New task: Create waits for a title; the task appears — 
- Chat ⋯ menu: every row; while replying the history rows are dimmed with a reason — 
- Rename from the menu; Session info; Copy session id; Compress; Undo — 
- Export as Markdown: the share sheet opens with a .md file — 
- Settings → Profiles: three profiles; switch with the circle; create "notes"; edit its SOUL; rename; delete by typing the name — 
- Search with focus: the scope bar appears; All profiles groups by profile; tapping a hit under another profile opens it — 

## Real server (hermes.shreyaskamat.in)
- Cold start: the tabs appear only if the server has the plugins — 
- Scheduled and Tasks load; pull to refresh; a poll updates the list within 15 s — 
- (Live write test already covered create/pause/trigger/delete.)

## Cross-cutting
- Light mode on every new screen — 
- 320 pt wide (use a small AVD or `wm size 720x1280` and `wm density 320`): nothing overflows — 
- Font size 200% (Settings → Display): nothing clips or overflows — 
- Reduced motion (Settings → Accessibility → Remove animations): the segmented thumb jumps, no ticker runs — 
- TalkBack names the segments, the switch states, the ⋯ button — 
- Cold restart keeps the theme and profile — 
```
